美股擺脫通膨疑慮，利率仍偏震盪格局
2026 年 6 月
5 月截至 22 日為止，全球股市表現(以美元計價)呈現高度分化，「AI 半導體」與「油價地緣政
治」兩股力道拉鋸，使各區域報酬差距明顯擴大。MSCI 新興亞洲與新興市場期間分別勁揚 7.1%與
5.4%，雙雙領先，主要受惠台灣與南韓在 AI 半導體供應鏈的強勁買盤 ，南韓今年漲幅已逾八成、
台灣約四成，為全球最熱的股市。成熟市場方面，標普 500 期間上漲 3.8%，MSCI 日本、歐洲分別
上漲 3%與 1.8%，均有不錯表現。相對地，MSCI 拉丁美洲單月下跌 4.3%，為主要市場中表現最弱，

主因年初大漲後，通膨上揚壓縮巴西後續降息空間及該國的大選選情變化導致投資人獲利了結。中國
與印度表現不佳，大致是受美伊衝突帶動國際油價走高的拖累。
相較於 4 月在美元走弱帶動下的全面反彈，5 月截至 22 日為止，全球債市明顯承壓，七大類債
券中六類收黑，主要仍是因美伊衝突推升國際油價，再度點燃通膨疑慮，全球債券殖利率走升所致。
以券種觀察，存續期間較長、利率敏感度較高的全球主權債券單月下跌  0.85%、全球投資級公司債
也下跌 0.47%；新興當地債亦由４月的領漲轉為小幅回檔，反映美元自５月中旬開始轉強，導致匯
率貢獻由正轉負。 亞洲高收益債則逆勢上漲  0.33%，為唯一收紅 券種，主要受惠較高的票息收益
（carry）與相對較短的存續期間，於殖利率走升環境中提供緩衝。整體而言，在地緣與通膨變數明
朗化之前，債市配置宜留意存續期間風險以強化防禦性。
儘管中東戰事推升通膨疑慮，但自 4 月起以來，原油、美股走向明顯分歧。美股在 AI 資本支出
題材與強勁財報帶動下改寫歷史新高，與過去地緣衝突常見的 「油漲股跌」模式背道而馳。美伊談判

進展反覆或使股市短期波動，但美國 11 月期中選舉將迫使川普在中東議題作出妥協，市場的通膨疑
慮可望下降，且 AI 題材對科技股的盈餘帶動也料持續，科技類股與台日股市都仍有向上潛力。
債市方面，美伊和談雖正向發展，但因雙方歧見仍存，短期內殖利率料維持高檔震盪。然而，目
前債券殖利率水準明顯高於歷史均值，我們認為站在息收角度，債券仍具長線投資價值。此外，投資
人對美債殖利率曲線陡峭化疑慮短期不會消退，加上長債存續期間偏高。因此，我們仍維持債券投資
聚焦中短天期公債/投資級債的建議。

美國股市：市場樂觀預期美伊協議達成，美股再創新高
五月美股聚焦於 AI 產業進展與供應鏈訊息，且投資人樂觀預期美伊有望達成協議，並使荷姆茲
海峽恢復正常通行， 推動標普與納指 再創歷史新高 。指數上揚主要由 蘋果、美光、超微半導體 、
Alphabet、特斯拉、禮來、輝達、英特爾、思科等科技股帶動，表現相對弱勢的族群包括 Meta、微
軟、沃爾瑪、萬事達卡、直覺、網飛、埃克森美孚、碩騰、新紀元能源、康菲石油等，市場焦點回歸
AI 贏家與受惠的供應鏈。
美國 5 月製造業 PMI 自上月的 54.5 上升至 55.3，服務業 PMI 自上月的 51.0 下降至 50.9，低於

市場預期的 51.2。企業活動維持溫和擴張，但產業表現分化，其中製造業受到企業補庫存帶動，而
服務業略微放緩，高物價與不確定性持續壓抑消費與企業支出意願。此外，就業市場同樣出現分化，
製造業就業小幅增加，但整體民間企業聘僱增速下降，企業招聘態度趨於保守。價格方面，供應鏈延
誤與能源價格上漲推升企業成本。綜合來看，雖然美國經濟的確具有韌性，但通膨壓力仍持續累積。
美國 5 月密大消費者信心指數顯示，由於高油價與通膨疑慮升溫，消費者信心再創新低。密大消
費者信心指數終值由初值的 48.2 下修至 44.8，現況指標由初值的 47.8 下修至 45.8，預期指標則由
48.5 下滑至 44.1。消費者 1 年期通膨預期上升至 4.8%，且 5-10 年中長期通膨預期則大幅升至 3.9%，
全面高於預期。油價與生活成本壓力持續惡化下，消費者不僅對當前經濟與財務狀況評價惡化，對未
來景氣與通膨前景亦轉趨悲觀。近期 FOMC 會議紀錄顯示部分官員已開始討論移除政策聲明中的寬
鬆傾向指引，反映聯準會內部同樣對通膨風險的警戒升溫，政策立場轉趨偏鷹。

AI 投資熱潮推動標普 500 企業現金配置從庫藏股轉向資本支出 。 市場共識預估AI 雲端業者(亞馬
遜、Alphabet、META、微軟、甲骨文)2026 年資本支出可望超過 7,000 億美元，為強化現金流量，
這些公司只好削減庫藏股。近期財報季已突顯由庫藏股轉向資本支出與研發的趨勢，大型科技巨擘除
非選擇大規模舉債，否則未來一年企業庫藏股動能恐難明顯回升，讓未來一年的股市波動性擴大。
目前標普 500 本益比已升至約 26 倍，前瞻本益比也在 22 倍位置，評價面已偏高，加上新任聯
準會主席就任後政策不確定性、美伊談判反覆、AI 主題擁擠度偏高等多重變數，市場拉回風險仍需
留意。然而基本面仍具支撐，故雖然短期需考量估值、通膨與地緣政治風險，但中長期則維持對美股
及 AI 產業正面看法，操作上建議以分批、長期角度布局。

日本股市：AI 題材提振日股表現；日本央行 6 月升息機率上升
五月日股受惠於 AI 產業表現強勁，日經 225 指數站上 6 萬 5 千點歷史新高。市場主要由軟銀、
村田製作所、瑞可利控股、瑞薩電子、鎧俠、東京威力科創、三菱 UFJ 金融、索尼、住友商事等公
司帶動，表現相對弱勢的族群包括三菱重工、愛德萬測試、藤倉、三井物產、豪雅、丸紅、任天堂、
豐田、Disco、富士通等。市場焦點回歸 AI 主題，以及升息可能帶動的金融股。
日本 4 月出口年增 14.8%。弱勢日圓對出口帶來挹注，而實質出口年增 3.4%、連續兩個月維持

正成長，顯示出口量的成長亦是推動出口上行因素之一 。引導出口成長的重要品項包含半導體電子零
組件及非鐵金屬，其中半導體等電子零組件連續八個月的雙位數成長，就是受惠於 AI 產業需求。
日本 4 月 CPI 年增幅由前月的 1.5%下降至 1.4%，扣除新鮮食品的 CPI 年增率則由 1.8%上升至
1.4%，本月通膨的走弱為政策性補貼所影響。其中商品價格年增率上升至 1.7%，服務價格年增率則
降至 0.9%。展望後市，日本政府很可能繼續以財政手段平滑 CPI 波動。首相高市早苗於 5/25 宣布，
為因應長期化的能源成本飆漲 ， 日本政府將編列規模約為3 兆日圓的追加預算案，實際內容包括汽油
補貼、電力及瓦斯費補貼。高市同時承諾不會增加整體政府借貸，試圖緩解財政壓力憂慮。
貨幣政策方面，市場預期日銀將在 6 月啟動升息。近期日銀委員小枝淳子談話，提及應以適當地
速度調升政策利率以因應物價上漲，認為中東局勢惡化導致物價上漲的風險正在增加，然而小枝淳子
於上月會議認為應維持政策利率不變。先前美國財長貝森特表示，日本經濟基本面強勁，日圓過度波

動並不可取，相信日本央行將會採取必要行動。市場預期日本央行於 6 月啟動升息(下次會議時間為
6/10~6/11)，全年至少有 2 碼升息空間。近期可觀察日本十年期公債殖利率飆升來到 2.7%，對照美
國十年期公債殖利率仍在 4.5%，雙方利差持續縮減，有利日圓守穩 160 關卡。
日本 4 月外國人入境人數年減 5.5%至 369.2 萬人次，中國訪日旅客年減 56.8%至 33.1 萬人，連
續五個月出現雙位數的衰退，反映自去年日中關係惡化，中國遊客數維持於 30 萬人左右區間，但整
體外籍觀光客仍在單月 350~380 萬人次的高檔位置，顯示對日本觀光產業造成的負面影響有限。
日本經濟受惠於 AI 產業擴張，加上日本政府對 17 項戰略產業政策支撐明確，然而股市短期面對
中東衝突不確定性及漲多拉回風險，建議投資人可留意中東情勢 、 補充預算審議進度及日銀6 月會議
釋放的政策訊號，把握利空拉回機會、以長線角度、分批佈局日股。

新興亞洲股市：半導體與油價角力，亞洲市場分化表現
亞洲股市 5 月因中東情勢的和緩在科技類股的帶領下反彈，截至 5/26，亞洲不含日本指數
(MXAPJ) 過去一個月上漲 5.59%。南韓股市漲幅居前，KOSPI 指數單月上漲 21.19%。台灣加權指
數表現居次，上漲 12.10%。中國股市表現平平，上證指數上漲 1.78%，國企指數不漲反跌，下跌
2.56%。MSCI 東協指數受惠於美伊情勢和緩，5 月上漲 2.15%，但各國表現不盡相同。原本受惠於
油價上漲的馬來西亞及印尼由漲轉跌，其中印尼股市遭到外資短時間內在股市大舉提款，股匯雙殺，

單月下跌 12.95%，亞股中最差。新加坡、泰國、菲律賓則有 3-6%的漲幅。印度 SENSEX 指數下跌
0.55%，印度作為能源淨進口國，5 月上旬高企的國際油價加劇了市場對其國內通膨的擔憂，使得印
度央行在貨幣政策操作上的降息空間受到壓制，也壓抑了股市。
5 月亞洲製造業呈現以科技出口為核心的兩極分化格局。台灣在 AI 算力與全球半導體供應鏈強
勁拉動下，PMI 飆升至 60.3 領跑全亞洲；南韓則在外需復甦與記憶體晶片需求帶動下展開 V 型反
彈，一路回升至 53.6。印度雖自高檔拉回至 54.3，經濟在高油價的干擾下雖能維持擴張，但卻連續
放緩 。東協因歐美消費降溫 、 通膨壓制 ， 動能連月放緩至 50.7 的榮枯線邊緣； 中國大陸則持續在 50.3
微幅擴張處橫盤拉鋸，反映整體內需復甦依舊沉悶。
亞洲經濟呈現典型的 「科技強、傳統弱」格局，台、韓、兩國受惠於結構性轉型紅利而氣勢如虹，
雖也共同面臨中東衝突引發的原物料與運費飆升等成本通膨隱憂，但影響有限；相反地，缺乏科技大
潮加持的東協、印度與中國，在全球高利率與需求放緩的環境下，正提早面臨景氣停滯的嚴峻挑戰。

台、韓兩國經濟擴張具韌性，顯示全球算力基礎建設正處於實質出貨期。由於地緣政治與中東情
勢陰霾未散，資金將更傾向流入具有「實質獲利高成長」與「定價能力」的東北亞半導體巨頭。台股
加權指數與南韓 KOSPI 的強勢格局具備基本面支撐，高昂的運費與原物料成本雖是隱憂，但先進製
程與高階記憶體具備將成本轉嫁給歐美科技巨擘的能力。印度 PMI 持續放緩，股市將受高油價壓制，
印度的核心邏輯在於 「長線結構性轉型」 。未來只要中東地緣情勢未進一步惡化，國際油價能逐步回
穩，印度央行的貨幣政策擔憂將減輕。預期印度股市在震盪調整之後，長線仍有投資機會。中國製造
業 PMI 與股市的橫盤疲軟顯示內需消費信心的提振需要更多時間。未來可能的發展仍維持「以時間
換取空間」的築底格局，指數上漲空間有限。

科技類股：AI 資本支出全線擴散，企業獲利上行週期確立多頭韌性
全球科技板塊在 AI 基礎設施資本支出持續投入的背景下，正經歷結構性的架構轉型與產業鏈整
體獲利及估值上調。從大型雲端 CSP 業者的長期資本支出規劃，到硬體、網通與半導體設備大廠的
板塊效應外溢，均顯示 AI 需求具有實質支撐。儘管總體經濟環境面臨美債殖利率上行與高利率環境
的挑戰 ， 但科技類股目前正處於明確的獲利上行週期 ，企業獲利的增長預期將有效緩解估值修正壓力 。
根據彭博智庫與 S&P Global 的最新預測，全球大型雲端業者在 2026 年的總資本支出預計將達

到 8,200 億美元，較先前預估的 7,500 億美元出現顯著上修。在 2026 至 2030 年的長線格局下，全
球六大領先 CSP 業者的累計 AI 資本支出將接近 5 兆美元。其中，亞馬遜在 2026 年的資本支出預計
以 2,000 億美元位居首位，微軟與 Alphabet 緊隨其後。雖然高額支出在短期內可能對自由現金流造
成暫時性壓抑，但隨著基礎建設規模擴大，預期將帶動獲利自2028 年起加速成長；亞馬遜亦設定於
2029 年達成 1 兆美元的營收目標。
而 5 月以來的財報數據也都顯示 ，AI 資本支出除了算力晶片之外，也進一步擴散至連接端與製造
設備端。其中輝達單季營收將挑戰 780 億至 800 億美元。毛利預計將穩定維持在 75%左右的高檔。
思科單季營收年增 12%至 158.4 億美元 ， 淨利年增35%至 33.7 億美元 。 受惠於大型雲端業者對基礎
建設的投入，思科將全年 AI 相關訂單預估由原本的 50 億美元大幅上調至 90 億美元。應用材料第 2
季營收達 79.1 億美元，獲利超市場預期，且第3 季財測達 89.5 億美元。執行長預期 2026 年設備業

務將成長三成以上，這些公司的財報都顯示 AI 投資由算力端向連接端及設備端外溢的趨勢明確，良
好的基本面已經成為科技類股的成長基石。
市場正擔憂債券殖利率走高與高利率環境對科技股估值造成的壓力，但我們認為，應從估值面與
企業獲利週期進行評估。當前的總體經濟與市場結構與 2021 年的緊縮週期有些類似，但存在本質上
的差異。在 2021 年前後，聯準會為快速飆升的通膨而啟動升息，科技股因而下跌，但當時那斯達克
100 指數的預估本益比高達 35 倍，且企業獲利正處於下行週期。而目前那斯達克 100 指數預估本益
比約僅為 25.6 倍低於當時，且企業獲利更明確處於上升週期。短線上，殖利率維持高檔確實會讓科
技股產生技術性的獲利了結與波動，但在長期 AI 資本支出結構性成長與整體企業獲利步入上升週期
的雙重支撐下 ， 高利率環境對科技股長線評價的威脅將逐漸緩解。 建議投資人關注整體產業獲利全面
提升的帶來的結構性機會。

台灣股市：先進製程帶動結構性擴張，類股良性輪動構築中長線多頭
台灣 4 月外銷訂單金額達 874.5 億美元，創歷年同月新高，年增率高達 48.1%。受到 AI、高效
能運算及雲端資料服務需求的強勁拉動 ， 其中電子產品與資訊通信產品訂單表現尤為顯著 ， 預期未來
數月外銷訂單可望穩定維持在每月 800 億美元的水準。在供應鏈「去中化」趨勢下，全球高端製造
需求正高度向台灣集中 。 半導體持續擴產亦帶動本土精密機械與檢測設備訂單增長 ， 動能已逐步外溢
至傳統產業，使整體經濟走向更全面的復甦。
在外銷訂單高度成長的背後，正是因為台積電在先進製程與封裝技術上確立了絕對領先地位帶動

了整個台灣上下游產業鏈。台積電技術論壇揭示，在 AI 驅動下，2030 年全球半導體市場規模將達
1.5 兆美元，其中 AI 將貢獻 55%以上的營收。目前全球雲端服務供應商（CSP）對輝達 Blackwell
架構的強烈需求，高度仰賴台積電先進封裝產能的釋放。台積電的 2 奈米（N2）製成即將量產，更
先進的製程預計於 2026 年下半年投產，並著手規劃埃米世代製程。反觀英特爾與三星目前仍在先進
製程節點與良率之間掙扎，台積電不僅在技術進程上領先一至兩個世代，更透過良率高達 98%的
CoWoS 先進封裝建立技術壁壘，短期內對手在技術成熟度、量產規模與客戶信任度上皆難以競爭。
基本面的強勁表現反映在台股的籌碼與盤勢結構上 。 隨著5 月 21 日輝達財報化解市場觀望心理 ，
三大法人全面翻多，單日合計買超達 850.51 億元，其中外資單日買超即佔了 603.41 億元，成交值
顯著放大 。 此波修正成功洗淨市場的短線浮額 。 資金從漲多的記憶體板塊撤出 ， 流入基期較低的面板、
載板、 被動元件等產業， 盤面呈現健康且偏多的類股輪動特徵 。 資金不再僅僅獨攬於少數AI 權值股，

而是向各受惠供應鏈上下游擴散，構築了多頭的廣度。
在地緣政治上，5 月川習會整體會談並無重大協議，中美脫鉤的大趨勢持續。中美雙方在台灣問
題上維持各自表述，中方警告「台獨」與台海和平水火不容；美方則重申對台政策維持不變，雙方沒
有重大改變。在全球 CSP 廠商對算力需求尚未觸頂的基本面下，台灣高端製造與半導體供應鏈的核
心地位 ， 並未受到政治會談結果的實質干擾 。 輝達財報行情與訂單表現依然反映出算力稀缺的市場共
識，地緣政治風險僅止於情緒面擾動，未動搖產業趨勢。 台灣在全球 AI 與高效能運算資本支出浪潮
中，憑藉半導體先進製程與封裝技術，展現出極強的定價權與不可替代性。這使得總體經濟指標表現
亮眼，且擴張動能正由科技業外溢至傳統產業。台股呈現健康的類股輪動格局，地緣政治政治雜音並
未動搖高端製造向台灣集中的趨勢，中長線維持看多格局。

拉美市場：巴西總統大選前料將震盪，後市中性看待
5 月以來拉美股市表現相對疲弱，主因隨著 10 月巴西總統大選逐步逼近，選情不確定性升溫，
導致最大經濟體巴西股市出現震盪，同時美伊和談狀況膠著，油價持續維持高檔，帶動整體拉美國家
通膨預期升溫，降息預期縮窄，股市上漲動能便受到壓抑。
回顧歷史，巴西股市於大選前半年走勢普遍不佳，過去五次選舉前六個月MSCI 巴西指數報酬中
位數為負 4%，其中 2014 年與 2022 年兩次大選年更以全年下跌作收，顯示選情雜音升高之際，投
資人多於投票前轉趨觀望。在大選過後，巴西股市報酬率則取決於是否由資本市場偏好的候選人當

選，若是，則股市大多上漲，若否，則股市大多下跌，巴西股市 5 月來的跌幅就與此相關。
本次巴西總統大選中，相對受到資本市場偏好的候選人波索納洛，近期捲入醜聞，導致其支持度
大幅滑落，其被指控與涉案巴西史上最大銀行詐欺案 Banco Master 的前執行長關係密切，並涉及收
受其資金用於拍攝家族紀錄片。受此影響，民調機構 AtlasIntel 5 月 19 日最新民調顯示，在第二輪
對決調查中，魯拉以 48.9%支持度領先波索納洛的 41.8%，波索納洛由原先小幅領先轉為落後約 7
個百分點。由於市場對偏左派的魯拉財政與政策路線疑慮較深，民調再度倒向魯拉，連帶導致巴西股
市表現承壓。
另一方面，受美伊戰爭影響，西德州原油價格持續維持高檔，帶動巴西輸入性通膨壓力上升，市
場對通膨回落的信心隨之轉弱。根據巴西央行截至 5 月 22 日的 Focus 市場調查，市場預期 2026 年
消費者物價指數預期中位數已由四週前的 4.86% 升至 5.04%，進一步突破 4.50%的通膨目標上緣；
受此影響，市場對 2026 年底 Selic 基準利率的預期亦由四週前的 13.00%上修至 13.25%，意味市場

預期下半年降息幅度僅約 1 個百分點，降息預期幅度持續收窄，連帶限制股市評價向上修正的空間。
然而儘管受到選舉及通膨升溫兩股承壓力量，高盛最新報告仍對於巴西股市中長線維持加碼評
等，看多理由在於，巴西股市對本地利率的負相關程度為新興市場中之最，在過去降息循環中，股市
於首次降息後往往出現強勁漲勢，而目前巴西市場仍處於降息循環中；此外，今年以來外資對巴西股
市淨流入居新興市場之冠，亦有利股市支撐。
展望後市，降息循環有利巴西股市支撐，然而考量巴西股市於大選前表現普遍不佳，同時油價高
檔推升通膨預期、降息幅度持續收窄的情況下，指數上檔空間恐受壓抑，故我們對於拉美市場後市目
前抱持中性看法。

能源類股：雖仍供不應求，惟油價料將隨談判進度震盪走低
當前油價與能源類股表現仍取決於美伊和談進展， 5 月 23 日川普宣稱一份結束戰事並重啟荷
莫茲海峽的備忘錄已大致談定，將採先確立大方向原則，後續再就細節分軌協商的模式來結束戰爭，
伊朗官媒指出，荷莫茲海峽通行與核子相關議題，將分別在備忘錄簽署後 30 天及 60 天進行談判，
談判期間美國將分階段豁免伊朗制裁。然而截至最新消息，美伊雙方在荷莫茲海峽主權與通行收費、
鈾濃縮是否完全停止等核心議題上仍存在明顯分歧，故該份備忘錄能否順利簽署仍具不確定性。
受到荷莫茲海峽持續封鎖影響，IEA（國際能源署）5 月月報指出，4 月全球石油供給量再度下

降每日 180 萬桶至每日 9,510 萬桶，下降主因波斯灣國家停產量持續上升，不過美國、巴西、加
拿大等國家持續增加出口，也為全球油市提供部分緩解，IEA 將 2026 年美洲供給成長上修逾每日
60 萬桶至平均每日 150 萬桶，大西洋盆地原油出口也自 2 月以來增加每日 350 萬桶。
此外全球石油需求量下滑，也部分抵銷供給缺口。IEA 將 2026 年全球石油需求下修至年減每
日 42 萬桶，較開戰前的預測減少了每日 130 萬桶，產業面看以石化與航空產業需求下滑幅度最
深，國家別中，中國 4 月海運原油進口量相較 2 月大幅減少每日 360 萬桶，日韓印亦分別縮減每日
190 萬、100 萬與 76 萬桶。全球石油需求下降，主因中東石油供給受阻，導致部分石化公司被迫
減產，此外部分也跟中國經濟表現疲弱，導致的需求下滑有關。
然而儘管全球石油需求量下滑 ， 非中東地區國家也持續增產 ， 全球石油市場仍面臨每日約750 萬
桶的供給缺口，受此影響， IEA 統計全球可見庫存 3 月減少 1.29 億桶，4 月再減 1.17 億桶，

高盛則預估 5 月以來全球可見石油庫存以每日 870 萬桶的創紀錄速度被消耗。
但值得注意的是，儘管全球石油市場面臨嚴重供不應求，理應有利油價上漲，然而整個 5 月西
德州原油價格卻仍維持在每桶 80 至 110 美元區間震盪，並未進一步上漲。高盛指出，油價上漲受
阻，主因經濟合作暨發展組織(OECD)國家以每日 130 萬桶速度釋出戰略石油儲備，填補了部分缺
口，同時中國原油進口量自 2 月 27 日以來大減每日 430 萬桶，使市場上對於可售原油的爭奪趨
緩，連帶使油價漲幅受到受抑。
展望後市油價，EIA（美國能源資訊署）預估布蘭特原油價格 5、6 月均價將約為 106 美元，
隨後續海峽通行恢復，第四季油價可望回落至每桶 89 美元，惟若海峽重開延後至六月底，則後續
油價將可能較此預測在上升每桶 20 美元。整體而言，後市油價及能源類股表現，仍將視美伊談判
進度而定，然而考量美伊戰事持續朝收斂方向前進，預料後續油價將隨談判進度震盪走低，相較不利
能源類股表現，在缺乏進一步利多支撐的狀況下，我們對於能源類股後市目前保守看待。

黃金類股：黃金表現仍承壓，惟全球央行買盤漸強
5 月受到美伊戰爭帶動美債殖利率上行影響，黃金表現仍承壓，然而我們觀察到來自全球央行的
買盤逐漸轉為強勁，顯示各國央行對於逢低增持黃金的興趣仍存，有利為金價帶來支撐，預料來自央
行的買盤將可使金價保持在區間內震盪整理，等待美伊衝突緩解，殖利率上行的壓力消退。
央行買盤方面，高盛於5 月報告大幅上修其央行購金量預估，主因其推估模型自 2025 年 8 月起
系統性低估各國央行購金量，經參數修正後，高盛預估各國央行購金量 12 個月移動平均值由原本估
計的每月 29 公噸調升至每月 50 公噸，單月修正幅度更大，1 月由 12 公噸上修至 66 公噸、2 月由 2

公噸上修至 41 公噸，2026 全年央行購金量則預估為平均每月 60 公噸。
世界黃金協會數據亦呼應此預估，根據世界黃金協會統計 ，2026 年第 1 季全球央行淨購金達 244
公噸，季增 17%，超越前季與五年均值。在已申報買方中，波蘭央行 Q1 增持 31 公噸為最大買家，
烏茲別克央行則增持 25 公噸、哈薩克央行 12 公噸緊隨其後。此外中國人行持續增持黃金動作也未
停歇，在 4 月中國人行單月增持 8 公噸黃金，創下 2024 年 12 月以來單月新高，並且是連續第 18
個月增持黃金儲備，帶動其官方持有量上升至 2,322 公噸，占外匯儲備 9%。從以上數據可以看出，
各國央行在近幾個月金價走疲的階段，仍持續加大力度增持黃金儲備。
在黃金 ETF 資金面亦可見到轉折出現的跡象，繼 3 月黃金 ETF 出現顯著資金流出後，4 月全球
實物黃金 ETF 轉為出現 66 億美元的資金流入，所有地區在均呈正向流入，推動全球黃金 ETF 黃金持
有量從 3 月底約 4,092 公噸反彈 45 公噸至 4,137 公噸，達到歷史第三高 ， 僅略低於2/27 創下的 4,176

公噸紀錄，其中歐洲基金表現最強，4 月流入 37 億美元使年初至今累計由負轉正，主要受地緣政治
風險升溫、英國央行立場不如預期鷹派及本地股市回跌驅動。亞洲則延續第 8 個月淨流入趨勢，4 月
增加 18 億美元，其中香港受惠於新 ETF 產品掛牌上市錄得 7.32 億美元單月歷史新高，中國在地緣
緊張 、 殖利率下行及官方持續宣布增持黃金的背景下亦流入 4.98 億美元。由黃金 ETF 數據亦可看出，
市場資金對於逢低增持黃金配置的興趣正在上升。
展望後市，在國際地緣政治紛擾不斷的狀況下 ，仍可預期央行買盤與個人投資組合中分散風險的
需求可望延續，有利為金價帶來支撐，然而在高油價將可能使利率維持在高檔較久的環境下，黃金
ETF 資金流入恐難以重現 2025 年的強度，故預期中短線上金價將可能持續維持在區間內震盪，惟若
後續美伊談判推展順利，隨荷莫茲海峽開放，油價回落將有利市場降息預期回升，屆時搭配來自央行
的買盤，將有望再度重啟黃金新一輪多頭走勢。

### Page 10

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
固定收益：市場觀望華許首秀，美債利率料陷整理走勢
自 2/28 美伊戰事爆發並導致荷莫茲海峽封鎖以來 ， 全球金融市場經歷自 1970 年代以來最嚴重
的供給端通膨衝擊。西德州原油與布蘭特原油價格的大幅飆升，引發市場對於通膨，乃至於 Fed 利
率政策方向轉變的疑慮，成為推升美債殖利率的核心驅動引擎。
這場由地緣政治引發的能源危機，並非僅侷限於美國單一國家，而是全球公債市場的普遍現象。
由於全球能源報價以美元計價 ， 且原油實體供給短缺屬於全球性危機 ， 主要已開發國家的公債殖利率
呈現極高相關性的同步上揚 ， 其中又以日本與英國的公債殖利率升幅最大 。 日本 75% 能源依賴中東

供給，不論在物價上或是財政體質上面臨不小壓力。
我們預期 6 月市場的總經焦點將在華許首次主持的 6/17 FOMC 利率會議。4 月下旬在分析華許
在參議院聽證會的發言時 ， 便已提出其主張可能導致 「美債殖利率陡峭化」 與 「債市波動性恐將加大」
的想法。彭博 4/30~5/13 調查顯示，118 名受訪者中有 64% 預期華許上任後，美債利率將出現某
種形式的陡峭化，其中熊市陡峭化（長率升速>短率升速）是主流觀點(佔比近 30%)。未來若債券殖
利率曲線呈現陡峭化(不論是熊市、牛市或是扭轉陡峭化)，短中天期的債券殖利率表現都將相對優於
長天期債券，加上短天期債券的存續期間本就低於長天期公債，因此當債市震盪時，短天期債券價格
表現將優於長天期債券。
巴克萊近期研究也顯示，當聯邦基金利率在 2-5% 的區間內波動，平均約為 3.6% 時，30 年
期公債殖利率與聯邦基金利率之間的利差為 170 個基點。假設長期中性利率在 3-4%的區間內（如
聯準會點陣圖所示），30 年期公債殖利率應在 4.7-5.7%區間。而目前約 5%水準約在區間相對偏低

的水準。巴克萊認為，在美國經濟韌性的背景下，目前30 年期公債殖利率還不算高，考慮到當前的
財政狀況和異常的不確定性，投資人可能需要更高的期限溢酬。若此說法成真，相較於短期債券，長
天期債券殖利率上升幅度更高(價格下跌幅度更大)。
雖然 5 月至今股市有所表現，但在中東地緣政治談判反覆，能源通膨預期以及聯準會偏鷹派立場
的交互牽引下，債券市場表現卻相對震盪，30 年期美債殖利率甚至一度觸及 2007 年以來的高位。
近來市場定價邏輯高度聚焦於「通膨風險與聯準會政策路徑」 ，債券殖利率 6 月仍將持續面臨高檔震
盪的壓力。目前債券殖利率水準明顯高於其歷史均值，我們認為站在息收 (Carry)的角度，債券仍具
長線投資價值。此外，由於預期美債殖利率曲線趨向陡峭化，再加上長天期公債本就有較高的存續期
間，故就債券價格來看，短天期公債/投資級債券的表現料將優於長天期公債/投資級債券，因此我們
仍維持債券投資聚焦中短天期公債/投資級債的建議。

2026 年 10 月 9 日 全9 頁

Indicators Update 
2026 年 8 月消費統計 
耐久財とサービスが弱く、総じて見れば前月から減少

経済調査部 エコノミスト 龐 鈞文

[要約] 
◼ 2026 年8 月の家計調査における二人以上世帯の実質消費支出は前月比＋0.1％と2 カ月
連続で増加した。 一方、複数の需要側統計を用いて補正した世帯消費動向指数（ CTI
ミクロ）で見た実質消費は同▲2.3％と 2 カ月ぶりに減少した。また、供給側統計の 1
つである商業動態統計では、CPI の財指数で実質化した小売販売額は同▲1.1％だった。
総じて見れば、8 月の個人消費は前月から減少したと判断される。 
◼ 先行きの個人消費は 底堅く推移するとみている 。名目賃金は春闘での高水準の賃上げ
や最低賃金の引き上げもあって上昇が続く見込みである。 一方、政府の物価高対策 の
効果の剝落などが物価を押し上げる要因となろう。加えて、原油の輸入価格は代替調
達もあって高止まりしており、幅広い分野の価格への波及を通じて個人消費を下押し
する可能性には引き続き注意が必要だ。

＜2026 年8 月の消費総括＞耐久財やサービスを中心に減少 
需要側統計である家計調査における二人以上世帯の実質消費支出は、前月比＋0.1％と2カ月
連続で小幅に増加した一方、複数の需要側統計を用いて補正した世帯消費動向指数（CTI ミク
ロ）で見た実質消費は同▲2.3％と 2 カ月ぶりに減少した（図表 1）。また、供給側統計の 1 つ
である商業動態統計では、CPI の財指数で実質化した小売販売額は同▲1.1％だった。総じて見
れば、2026年8月の個人消費は前月から減少したと判断される。

＜CTI ミクロ・家計調査（需要側）＞「交通・通信」などの7 費目が減少 
2026年 8 月の CTIミクロ（二人以上の世帯）を費目別に見ると、10 大費目1のうち、 「交通・
通信」 （前月比▲5.3％） 、「その他」 （同▲4.1％）、 「教養娯楽」 （同▲2.9％） 、 「教育」 （同▲
4.7％） 、「家具・家事用品」 （同▲4.4％） 、「被服及び履物」 （同▲3.7％） 、「保健医療」 （同▲
0.5％）の7費目が減少した。 
他方、「光熱・水道」 （前月比＋4.4％） 、「住居」 （同＋3.3％） 、「食料」 （同＋0.5％）の3費目
が増加した（図表2）。

続いて、CTIミクロの10大費目に含まれる個別品目への支出について確認する。 
CTI ミクロにおける「交通・通信」は 2 カ月ぶりに減少した。自動車等関係費などへの支出
が減少した。「その他」は2カ月ぶりに減少した。諸雑費やこづかいなどへの支出が減少した。
「教養娯楽」は2カ月ぶりに減少した。教養娯楽サービスを中心に、教養娯楽用品などへの支出
が減少した。 「教育」は2 カ月連続で減少した。授業料等を中心に支出が減少した2。「家具・家
事用品」は2カ月ぶりに減少した。家庭用耐久財を中心に支出が減少した。「被服及び履物」は
2 カ月ぶりに減少した。洋服を中心に支出が減少した。「保健医療」は 2 カ月連続で減少した。
保健医療サービスを中心に支出が減少した。 
他方、「光熱・水道」は 2 カ月ぶりに増加した。電気代やガス代を中心に支出が拡大した。
「住居」は 2 カ月ぶりに増加した。家賃地代を中心に支出が拡大した。「食料」は 2 カ月連続で
増加した。酒類や野菜・海藻を中心に支出が拡大した。 
家計調査における二人以上世帯の実質消費支出は、前月比＋0.1％と2カ月連続で小幅に増加
した。ただし、振れの大きい項目を除いた、住居等を除くベースでは同▲1.7％と2カ月ぶりに
減少した（図表 3 上段左）。 
基礎的支出は前月比＋0.9％と、4 カ月ぶりに増加した。また、選択的支出は同＋1.3％と、2
カ月連続で増加した（いずれも大和総研による季節調整値、図表3 中段左）。 
消費支出を財・サービス別に見ると（大和総研による季節調整値、図表 3 中段右）、耐久財
（前月比＋8.2％）が4カ月ぶりに増加し、全体を押し上げた。非耐久財（同＋0.4％）も3カ月
ぶりに増加した。一方、サービス（同▲0.3％）と半耐久財（同▲0.1％）は減少した。

2 ただし、授業料等は児童や学生がいる一部の世帯以外はほとんど支出しないため、サンプルサイズが小さく
振れが大きい。

＜商業動態統計（供給側）＞小売販売額は名目・実質ともに2 カ月ぶりに減少 
2026 年 8 月の商業動態統計によると、名目小売販売額は前月比▲1.2％と 2 カ月ぶりに減少
した（図表 4、5） 。また、CPIの財指数で実質化した小売販売額は同▲1.1％だった。 
名目小売販売額を業種別に見ると、全ての業種が前月から減少した。「機械器具小売業」 （前
月比▲6.9％）は 2 カ月ぶりに減少した。「自動車小売業」 （同▲2.5％）は 4 カ月連続で減少し
た。8 月の新車販売台数（大和総研による季節調整値）は同▲7.6％と減少しており3、この結
果とも整合的だ。「燃料小売業」 （同▲1.8％）は2カ月ぶりに減少した。「飲食料品小売業」 （同
▲0.4％）は2カ月ぶりに減少した。コンビニの加工食品（同▲1.5％）が減少した。「織物・衣
服・身の回り品小売業」 （同▲1.8％）、「その他小売業」 （同▲0.2％）、「各種商品小売業」 （同
▲0.4％）はいずれも2カ月ぶりに減少した。

の小売業」 。

＜2026 年9 月の消費＞8 月から減少した可能性 
業界統計や個社データ、JCB 消費 NOW をもとに判断すると、2026 年 9 月の消費は 8 月から減
少したとみている4。財消費では、9 月前半の実績をもとに試算したスーパーの JCB 消費額（大
和総研による季節調整値）と家電の JCB 消費額（同）は減少した。一方、アパレルの売上高は
堅調に推移し、新車販売台数（同）も増加した。サービス消費では、宿泊の JCB 消費額（同）
と外食の JCB 消費額（同）は天候不順もあっていずれも前月から減少した。一方、新幹線輸送
量の前年比伸び率は前月から拡大した。

＜先行き＞個人消費は底堅く推移する見込み／価格上昇の広がりに注意 
先行きの個人消費は底堅く推移するとみている。名目賃金の伸びが高水準で推移する一方、
政府の物価高対策による物価の押し下げ効果の剝落などで、物価上昇率が高まり、実質賃金が
伸び悩む可能性がある。 
名目賃金の伸びは高水準で推移するだろう。春闘の結果5は前年をやや下回るものの、高水準
となった。また、 2026 年度の最低賃金の 引き上げ率は全国加重平均で＋ 5.0％（前年度は＋
6.3％）と高水準を維持した6。 
消費者物価について、7 月使用分から 9 月使用分まで実施される電気・ガス料金の補助とい
った政府の物価高対策が終了した。加えて、代替調達もあって原油の輸入価格は高止まりして
おり7、原材料費や輸送費の上昇を通じて幅広い分野の価格に波及し、消費を下押しする可能性
には注意が必要だ。帝国データバンクによると、値上げされた飲食料品のうち中東情勢の影響
を受けた品目の割合は 9 月末時点で約 3 割だった。今後も値上げが継続する見込みだが、値上
げの品目数は10月で一旦ピークアウトする可能性もある8。 
また、金利上昇による住宅ローン等の利払い負担増が個人消費にもたらす悪影響にも警戒す
る必要がある9。さらに、消費動向調査（内閣府）によると、9 月の消費者態度指数（二人以上
の世帯、季節調整値 ）は 35.4（前月差▲0.1pt）と小幅に低下し、依然として低水準にあり
（巻末図表「消費者マインド」参照） 、節約志向の高まりに注意が必要だ。

4 詳細は、拙稿「消費データブック（2026/10/6 号）」（大和総研レポート、2026 年10月 6 日）を参照。 
5 日本労働組合総連合会（連合）「3 年連続の 5％超え！～2026 春季生活闘争 第7 回（最終）回答集計結果
について～」（2026 年7 月3日）によると、定昇相当込みの賃上げ率の平均は 5.01％（前年同時期は
5.25％）となった。 
6 厚生労働省「地域別最低賃金の全国一覧」（2026 年10 月9 日閲覧） 
7 世界的な需給の引き締まりも受け、ホルムズ海峡を経由しないルートでの中東や他地域からの原油の代替調
達の価格が割高となり、調達コストが押し上げられている。詳細は、神田慶司・畑中宏仁・中村華奈子・横田
凱「日本経済見通し：2026 年8 月」（大和総研レポート、2026 年8 月 24 日）を参照。 
8 帝国データバンク「『食品主要 195 社』価格改定動向調査 ― 2026 年 10 月 飲食料品値上げ、10 月は3153
品目 値上げラッシュはピーク越え」（2026 年9 月30 日）

2026 年8 月24 日）を参照。

### Page 1

Telecom & Networking Equipment  | North America
Potential FCC Rules on Optical
Transceivers More Likely to
Come in at 3.2T
Recent DC meetings highlight 3.2T as the most likely generation
to see restrictions on China-based optical transceivers. This
echoes conversations with LITE during the Q noting that any
restrictions would need to be phased in. See potential rules
keeping laser market tight, a pos for LITE/COHR.
Morgan Stanley & Co. LLC

Morgan Stanley MUFG Securities Co., Ltd.+
Yu Shirakawa
Equity Analyst
Yu.Shirakawa@morganstanleymufg.com                                                +81 3 6836-5432
Telecom & Networking Equipment
North America
Industry View In-Line
What Happened: Morgan Stanley strategists recently  met with a senior Washington
telecom, FCC and export-control legal team with direct engagement across the
White House and FCC. Discussion focused on the Administration’s efforts to reduce

reliance on Chinese technology, with the FCC Covered List emerging as the more
likely near-term mechanism for optical transceivers (a note we made in our AI Safety
DC trip wrap-up last week). Policy discussions suggest 3.2T transceivers would likely
be where restrictions would be phased in, potentially as soon as October, with
industry recently asked to demonstrate credible U.S. or friend-shoring plans before
3.2T becomes broadly deployed. Our conversations noted that discussions are also

indicating Chinese-built transceivers could still be importable, even at 3.2T, as long
as 65% of the BOM comes from US-based companies (DSP / laser would get you
pretty close). Initial 3.2T volumes are not expected to ramp in volume until 2029,
limiting the near-term revenue impact of a restriction even if action comes sooner.
We would see these rules as having the potential to avoid creating a constraint
on hyperscalers' ability to build today, but keeping the  laser market tighter given

laser would largely need to be from a US-based vendor to meet a 65% value
threshold, a positive for LITE/COHR.
How this echoes conversation with LITE earlier in the quarter: We hosted LITE at
Nasdaq Asia in late August, where we asked the company about potential FCC rules.
At the time they noted that the FCC had been in contact with them about potential
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Recent DC trip by our strategist   team gave a greater idea of what potential FCC
rules on optical transceivers could look like.
Potential restrictions, likely to come in at 3.2T, limit Chinese-based transceivers,
with a potential workaround if US-based content above 65% of the BOM.
Solution seemingly a win-win for LITE/COHR, with laser market likely to stay

tight, but not having to take on lower module assembly business.
Also, does not create bottlenecks for hyperscalers.
Assuring InP substrate delivery will be key to look at going forward.
October 1, 2026 04:09 AM GMT

### Page 2

M
Update
2
rules, with the company noting their feedback was that the rules would need to be
phased in over time to prevent material supply/demand dislocation in the market
with 60-65% of transceivers coming from China-based transceiver vendors. They
also noted that pure transceiver volumes alone, at current pricing structures, would
not be the most attractive business for them to bring on. These rules, which are
both phased in and don't limit Chinese assemblers, as long as enough content is

US-based, would seemingly address both of these concerns.
Our Thoughts:
1. 3.2T looks like the likely policy pressure point, but the near-term impact
should be limited - Administration efforts appear focused on reshaping the
supply chain before 3.2T reaches volume rather than disrupting the existing
800G and 1.6T base. With the 3.2T ramp not expected to begin until 2028
and broader adoption closer to 2029, an October Covered List action would
provide roughly two years before meaningful volumes enter the market.

Hyperscalers are already working on plans intended to demonstrate greater
U.S. or friend-shored manufacturing, suggesting the policy objective is
increasingly about determining where the next generation is built rather
than immediately displacing current Chinese supply.
2. Laser pricing looks like the stronger read-through versus transceiver
share - A Covered List framework does not necessarily require Chinese
module vendors to lose significant share if they can increase the U.S.

content of the underlying BOM. Combining a U.S. DSP from Marvell with
laser content from LITE/COHR could potentially move a Chinese-built
transceiver to the 65% U.S.-value threshold discussed in our strategists
team policy meeting. Such an outcome would leave Innolight and Eoptolink
(both covered by Andy Meng) with less flexibility to substitute toward
China-based lasers while allowing them to remain relevant at the module
level, reducing the risk from China-based laser makers. We therefore see a

stronger read-through for high-speed laser pricing and LITE/COHR
component share than for a wholesale redistribution of transceiver share.
3. Replacement capacity not ready; nor is it the most attractive opportunity
- As noted above, LITE noted that assembly of transceivers (mid-30's to low-
40s gross margin product), is not the most attractive opportunity for US-
based optical companies (vs. EMLs and lasers well north of 50% steady
state and approaching 75% today). While 3.2T is not yet in production, we

use AAOI's current 800G and 1.6T capacity ramp as an illustration of the
scale of US supply available to absorb a potential future demand shift. AAOI
(not covered), the only material US-based production of transceivers, is
currently ramping capacity. AAOI exited Q2 with manufacturing capacity
approaching 200,000 800G and 1.6T units per month and expects to reach
~650,000 units per month by year-end 2026, while management continues
to expect demand to outpace production through mid-2027. AAOI’s YE26

capacity annualizes to ~12M 800G/1.6T units, versus ~101M units
combined for Innolight and Eoptolink based on annualized 1H capacity
(44.3M and 56.7M, respectively), or >8x AAOI. Based on AAOI’s year-end
capacity plans and our Internet team’s hyperscaler capex work, we estimate
AAOI could support less than 4 GW of hyperscaler capacity even before

Morgan Stanley Research 3
factoring in manufacturing utilization. Replacing a meaningful portion of
Innolight and Eoptolink’s supply would therefore require a substantial multi-
year capacity build, with limited ability for existing U.S. suppliers to absorb
those volumes in the near term.
4. InP supply and sourcing outside of China will still be areas to watch -
Potential FCC rules could create a win-win for LITE/COHR by reducing the
risk of China-based laser makers flooding the market, without forcing them

to build significant transceiver module capacity to absorb lower-margin
assembly business. Were China to view the rules as too one-sided, it could
respond with tighter restrictions on InP substrate exports, a key bottleneck
to laser capacity ramping. At the same time, sourcing outside of China could
broaden the beneficiary set beyond U.S. suppliers, supporting InP substrate
demand at JX Advanced Metals (5016 JP), while providing a tailwind for
Sumitomo Electric’s (5802 JP) CWLD products and Furukawa Electric’s

(5801 JP) DFB lasers.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over
the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.

Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.

75" 4K 65" 4K 55" FHD 50" FHD 43" FHD 32" HD
Source:                         Trendforce, Morgan Stanley Research
TV panel prices to stay  flat MoM in October: Mainstream 32", 43", 55", 65" and 75"
TV panel prices are expected to stay flat for the month of October. While panel
makers had previously notified their brands customers of potential price hikes from
4Q26 to mitigate elevated component costs, we believe that negotiations are
ongoing, as TV panel order momentum might slow down in the coming months on

seasonality and TV brands could reduce orders on higher panel prices. On the other
hand, panel makers continue to demonstrate good discipline, since Chinese panel
makers are committed to annual maintenance during the October holiday to control
output. We think it seems more likely that we will see TV panel price hikes toward
late 4Q26 when TV brands start to restock for CNY promotions. Our base-case
assumption is for the TV panel prices  to be flat QoQ in 4Q26, on a quarterly average

basis.
IT panel prices to remain flat MoM in October: Monitor and notebook panel prices
are expected to be flat MoM during the month. Both applications continue to  see
slower demand in  2H26 post the earlier restocking  in 1H26 amid concerns about
increasing component costs. That said, panel makers are under cost pressure and
reluctant to given more price concessions. On a quarterly average basis, we expect
IT panel prices to remain flat QoQ in 4Q26.

Risk/reward  less attractive: We maintain the view that the current valuation of
Taiwanese panel makers, AUO (2409.TW) at 2.0x P/B and Innolux (3481.TW) at 1.7x
P/B, have already baked in the expectations of glass in advanced packaging for
revenue contribution in 2028. However, some supply-chain players think this might
take longer, and there seem to be more suppliers from different supply chains vying
for this opportunity. In China, while expectations are also building for advanced

packaging, we have a    relative preference for BOE (000725.SZ; 1.4x P/B) over TCL
(000100.SZ; 1.7x P/B), on its scale  and faster progress in glass core substrates.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley
Research as only a single factor in making their investment

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
We believe current valuations of 1.5-2.0x P/B have already priced in expectations
of glass in advanced packaging from 2H28 for the panel makers.
Nonetheless,  the risk in commercialization and competition remain key to watch.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
TV Panel Prices Trended Down in 3Q26
TV panel prices started to edge up at the beginning of this year, rising 1% MoM in January,
2% MoM in February and 2% MoM in March. April, May and June were largely flat, while
July was down 2% MoM, August was down 1% MoM, and September was flat MoM,   in line
with our expectation that TV panel prices would start trending down from 3Q26.  That
said, we do not expect a significant decline as supply-side discipline should provide

downside support.

Below-seasonal Panel Shipments in 2H26
Over the past decade, TV panel shipments have typically been split 49%:51% between 1H
and 2H. In 2026, we expect the mix to skew toward 1H, as TV brands restocked ahead of
major promotional events, including the Winter Olympics in February, the FIFA World Cup
in June-July, and China's 618 shopping festival. We see no clear evidence that these events

will materially boost global TV demand, but they have historically shifted shipment timing
across quarters. As a result, we expect the restocking momentum seen since the start of
the year to fade, with panel order momentum slowing in 2H26.
Display Fab Utilization  ~85% in 3Q26
Stronger TV panel shipments supported display fab utilization of ~80% in 4Q24 and 82%
in 1Q25. Utilization declined to 78% in 2Q25 as brands adopted a more cautious stance
following earlier pull-ins and as the impact of China's trade-in program moderated.

Utilization rebounded to 80-85% in 3Q25 as brands restocked for year-end promotions
and remained at similar levels in 4Q25 despite seasonal softness as well as in 1Q26.
Industry utilization stayed broadly flat QoQ, at 80-85% in 2Q26, reflecting continued
supply discipline, followed by a slight uptick in 3Q26.
Over the medium to long term, we continue to believe the display industry is undergoing
structural change. Chinese panel makers have shifted their focus from top-line growth to

profitability, while lower government subsidies have reinforced supply discipline. We
believe investors increasingly recognize that the industry's production-to-order model can
remain sustainable, particularly as panel price cycles shortened to quarters in 2023-24 vs.
multiyear cycles in the previous decade. Consolidation actions, including LG Display's sale
of its Gen 8.5 Guangzhou fab to TCL and Sharp's closure of its Gen 10 Sakai fab, also

support a healthier supply backdrop (for more information, see Display – Structural
Changes on Supply Side Not Fairly Priced In, June 17, 2021).

Morgan Stanley Research 5
Risk/Reward Largely Fair
We view risk-reward as broadly balanced following the recent correction in panel stocks.
Below are our current views:
• AUO (2409.TW, Derrick Yang): We rate AUO Equal-weight with a PT of NT
$27.00, based on a 1.4x 2026e P/B multiple. While we believe the current round of
TV panel price increases is nearing an end, major panel makers should continue to
demonstrate solid supply discipline, providing downside support. We appreciate

AUO's efforts to diversify its business, but meaningful earnings contributions are
likely to take time.
• BOE (000725.SZ, Derrick Yang): We prefer BOE due to its scale advantage,
higher exposure to display and faster progress in glass core substrates. Our PT of
Rmb9.30 is based on 2.5x 2026e P/B, which we view as reasonable given our
estimated ROE of 5-8% over 2026-28 compared with BOE's average P/B of 1.1x and
ROE of 2-4% during 2023-25.
• Innolux (3481.TW, Derrick Yang): In semiconductors, our supply-chain checks

suggest that Innolux is participating in a foundry customer's glass core substrate
project. However, we do not expect meaningful contributions before 2028, and
additional competitors could enter as the industry moves toward mass production.
We maintain our Equal-weight rating and PT of NT$60.00, based on a 2.1x 2026e
P/B multiple.
• LG Display (034220.KS, Ryan Kim): Meaningful OLED revenue contribution
should increasingly improve earnings from 2H25, but potentially weaker end-

markets keep us from turning bullish on LGD. The stock trades at 0.6x 2026e P/B
vs. a historical mid-cycle level of 0.5x.
• TCL (000100.SZ, Derrick Yang): We are EW with a price target of Rmb4.70 (1.7x
2026e P/B). The stock is trading at 1.7x 2026e P/B vs. its mid-cycle average of 1.4x
since 2022.
• Greater China DDIC supply chain implications – Novatek (3034.TW, Daniel Yen):
Our Underweight thesis reflects weaker PC, smartphone and automotive demand,

gross margin pressure, and a lack of new growth drivers. That said, Novatek's
OLED DDI roadmap appears more competitive than those of its key Korean peers
in terms of both performance and cost, helping it gain share in Apple's iPhone
supply chain.

### Page 7

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 7
Where We Could Be Wrong
• Stronger end demand: If end demand for major applications turns out to be better
than expected, there could be stronger panel shipments and less panel price
pressure.
• More stringent output control: If major panel makers collectively adjust
utilization to an extreme extent, they could mitigate pricing pressure in the case of
softer demand.
• Non-commodity display business: If the non-commodity business progresses

faster than expected, panel makers could show better financial results.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over
the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.

Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Document 156: AI交易逻辑生变！从日韩到美国，全球芯片股惨跌，明星IPO搁浅、长鑫技术突破接连敲警钟

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI交易逻辑生变！从日韩到美国，全球芯片股惨跌，明星IPO搁浅、长鑫技术突破接连敲警钟.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-13
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

在今年涨幅显著的 AI 相关股票中，存储芯⽚股尤其容易成为获利了结的对象；在交易拥
挤、估值偏⾼的情况下，新的竞争信号⾜以引发集中抛售。华尔街⼈⼠称，⻓鑫科技的 4F²
DRAM 架构让投资者借机获利了结，其技术进展确有重⼤意义，但不能直接视为⽴即威胁
现有⼚商； AI ⾏情并未终结，可能进⼊下⼀阶段，投资者更关注收益兑现、⽽⾮纯押注基
础设施扩张。
摘要：
10 ⽉ 13 ⽇周⼆， AI 芯⽚股抛售潮继续蔓延，从韩国、⽇本到美国，全球半导体板块整体重挫。
韩国综合股价指数（ KOSPI ）收跌 5.5% ，⽇本存储芯⽚制造商铠侠（ Kioxia ）跌超 10% 。美股
盘中，费城半导体指数（ SOX ）⼀度跌超 3% ，美光科技（ MU ）⼀度跌超 6% ，闪迪（ SNDK ）、
希捷科技（ STX ）和西部数据（ WDC ）也重挫，刷新⽇低时⾄少跌近 6% 。
午盘美股芯⽚股跌势缓和，到收盘，费城半导体指数跌约 0.2% ，美光跌约 3.6% 、西部数据跌约
1.6% 、希捷科技跌 0.8% ，闪迪跌约 0.3% 。

这轮抛售并⾮由单⼀因素驱动。路透社分析指出， AI 基础设施投资热潮持续近四年后，市场情绪
正发⽣变化：利率上升、债券市场波动加剧、 AI 融资成本攀升，以及巨额资本开⽀能否兑现为实
际收益，均开始受到更严格的审视。
上周，英伟达持股的数据中⼼运营商 Firmus 搁置了原计划在澳⼤利亚进⾏的 50 亿美元 IPO ；同
时，中国存储芯⽚⼚商⻓鑫科技披露 4F² DRAM 架构研发进展，进⼀步触发市场对存储⾏业竞争
格局的担忧。
⻓鑫的技术进展可能成为本轮存储股抛售的直接催化剂，但更深层的变化是，投资者开始从追
逐 AI 增⻓转向审视资本回报。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

华尔街⼈⼠指出，在今年涨幅显著的 AI 相关股票中，存储芯⽚股尤其容易成为获利了结的对象；
⽽在交易拥挤、估值偏⾼的情况下，新的竞争信号⾜以引发集中抛售。不过，也有分析⼈⼠认
为，⻓鑫距离⼤规模量产仍有差距，尤其在⾼带宽内存（ HBM ）等先进领域，尚不⾜以对韩国
⼚商构成⽴即性的重⼤威胁。
AI 交易逻辑⽣变：从追逐增⻓到追问回报
⾃ OpenAI 推出 ChatGPT 以来，资⾦持续涌⼊半导体公司、数据中⼼运营商、电信基础设施企
业，以及其他受益于 AI 算⼒建设的公司。这场投资热潮推动芯⽚股⼤幅上涨，也带动数据中⼼建
设和服务器等基础设施的资本开⽀不断扩⼤。
但如今，这些交易的裂痕正在不同市场间蔓延。路透指出，相关资产的调整迹象早在 6 ⽉就已出
现，近期⼜延伸⾄⼀级市场和信贷市场。上周，英伟达⽀持的数据中⼼运营商 Firmus 因投资者
兴趣减弱，搁置了原计划在澳⼤利亚进⾏的 50 亿美元 IPO ，凸显出资本市场对 AI 基础设施项⽬的
⻛险评估正在趋严。
债券市场同样传递出谨慎信号。随着 AI 相关融资需求增加，投资者开始更加关注企业融资成本及

其偿债能⼒。腾讯控股周⼆在⾹港发⾏美元债券和点⼼债，其 10 年期美元债的发⾏指导收益率
较当时美国国债收益率⾼出约 1.15 个百分点。⽇本科技投资公司软银上⽉发⾏了规模达 45 亿美
元的⾼收益债券，期限为七年半，承诺⽀付最⾼ 9.75% 的利息。
利率与债券波动性成为市场重新定价的重要推⼿。悉尼威尔逊资产管理公司投资组合策略师达
⽶安 · 博伊表⽰，关键变化不仅是债券收益率上升，还包括债券市场波动加剧，这种变化正逐渐
传导⾄各类资产的定价。他认为， AI 领域的资本开⽀热潮可能有所降温，部分参与者也可能因此
受到冲击。
⻉莱德研究部⻔周⼀将美国股市展望下调⾄ “ 中性 ” ，理由是 AI 基础设施建设可能引发市场波动，
并转⽽看好短期美国国债。国际货币基⾦组织（ IMF ）周⼆也警告， AI 市场调整的影响可能波及
全球。尽管 AI 投资有望推动经济增⻓，但相关投资能否产⽣与预期相匹配的收益，仍存在不确定
性。
这意味着，市场关注点正在发⽣变化：此前，投资者主要押注算⼒需求持续增⻓及相关企业扩

⼤资本开⽀；如今，资本成本、融资可持续性和投资回报率，正成为影响估值的重要变量。
从⽇韩到美国，芯⽚股遭遇跨市场抛售
周⼆亚洲市场的跌势延续⾄美国。韩国综合股价指数（ KOSPI ）盘中跌幅⼀度超过 6% ，连跌五
个交易⽇；东京上市的存储芯⽚制造商铠侠收跌逾 13% ；韩股三星电⼦和 SK 海⼒⼠分别收跌超
7% 和 10% 。
美国芯⽚股同样承压。费城半导体指数（ SOX ）周⼀收跌约 2.7% ，周⼆盘中再度下探，早盘刷
新⽇低时⽇内跌超 3% ，势将连续五个交易⽇收跌、创将近 17 个⽉来最⻓连跌⽇。
存储及数据存储相关个股跌幅尤为显著。美光科技（ MU ）盘中⼀度跌超 6% ，盘中刷新⽇低
时，希捷科技（ STX ）跌近 8% ，西部数据（ WDC ）跌超 7% ， SK 海⼒⼠美股（ SKHY ）跌超
6% ，闪迪（ SNDK ）跌近 6% 。
这轮调整之所以引⼈关注，在于相关股票此前已积累可观涨幅。截⾄本周⼀收盘，费城半导体
指数今年以来仍有约 72.8% 的累计涨幅，美光股价今年内累涨超 240% 。此前强劲的上涨，使得

投资者对估值、持仓集中度和潜在竞争⻛险更加敏感。

### Page 3

从韩国存储巨头到美国芯⽚股，抛售范围不断扩⼤，也说明市场担忧已不限于某⼀家企业的技
术进展。投资者正重新审视整个 AI 硬件产业链的估值基础，以及持续增加的资本投⼊能否带来⾜
够的利润和现⾦流。
⻓鑫技术突破引发担忧，华尔街称投资者借机获利了结
⻓鑫科技的 4F² DRAM 架构研发进展，为本轮存储股调整提供了新的催化剂。
据上证报报道，⻓鑫科技总裁曹堪宇在 10 ⽉ 9 ⽇⾄ 10 ⽇举⾏的第四届集成芯⽚和芯粒⼤会上介绍
了公司在 DRAM 器件、 4F² 架构及混合键合等⽅向的技术探索，并宣布预计于今年年底推出融合
相关技术的新⼀代 DDR5 RDIMM 服务器内存产品。
⽬前， DRAM ⾏业主流存储单元采⽤ 6F² 架构。⻓鑫研发的 4F² 架构则通过⾃研的 VSG （垂直包
围栅）晶体管改变存储阵列的布局，以加强栅极控制、改善漏电问题并缩⼩存储单元⾯积，从
⽽提⾼存储密度。这⼀⽅向若能实现稳定量产，将为中国⼚商进⼀步进⼊服务器内存市场提供
新的技术基础。
但⻓鑫的进展究竟会在多⼤程度上冲击现有存储巨头，市场并没有⼀致判断。

Dakota Wealth 的⾼级投资组合经理 Robert  Pavlik 将本轮⾏情形容为获利了结潮。他表⽰，当投
资者意识到潜在竞争可能到来时，市场参与者可能同时涌向出⼝。这⼀⽐喻反映出，在交易拥
挤的板块中，即便新的竞争威胁尚未转化为实际市场份额变化，也可能迅速触发集中减仓。
媒体分析则强调，⻓鑫在技术上取得进展确实具有意义，但不宜将其直接等同于对现有⼚商的
即时威胁。
Rayliant Global Advisors ⾸席市场策略师 Phillip Wool 认为，⻓鑫的技术进展是中国芯⽚产业的
重要时刻，但周⼆海外芯⽚股抛售的规模可能与其近期实际威胁并不相称。他指出，⻓鑫尚未
证明能实现⼤规模量产，市场份额也远低于三星电⼦和 SK 海⼒⼠。更重要的是，当前讨论的主
要是常规 DRAM 架构，⽽⾮ HBM ；在可预⻅的未来，韩国⼚商在 HBM 领域仍占据明显优势。
瑞银⾼级股票顾问 Vey-Sern Ling 也表⽰，投资者正在寻找获利了结的理由，尤其是对于今年以
来表现突出的 AI 相关股票和存储股。中国⼚商在 HBM 等先进领域仍有相当⻓的路要⾛。

这两类观点并不⽭盾：⻓鑫的技术进展可能改变投资者对未来竞争的预期，但并不意味着其已
具备全⾯挑战国际存储巨头的能⼒。对于此前涨幅巨⼤的存储股⽽⾔，竞争预期变化可能成为
触发减仓的理由，⽽⾮当前盈利基本⾯已经发⽣逆转的确凿证据。
⻢斯克周⼀也在社交平台评论中国存储芯⽚产业的发展。他表⽰，当中国掌握某种产品的制造
⽅法后，其规模化⽣产能⼒往往可能超过世界其他地区，并以锂电池和太阳能产业为例。他还
称，中国似乎正在迅速攻克 LP5 和 LP6 等通⽤内存规格。

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
不过，技术研发取得进展、产品推出、通过客⼾验证和实现⼤规模量产，是不同阶段。⻓鑫的
进展强化了市场对竞争加剧的关注，但现有披露尚不⾜以证明其已经在⾼端服务器内存市场形
成⾜以改变⾏业格局的商业竞争⼒。
资本成本与 AI 投资回报，成为市场新的考题
芯⽚股的调整只是更⼴泛变化的⼀部分。随着 AI 基础设施建设持续消耗资⾦，市场对相关企业融
资成本、投资规模和未来现⾦流的关注也在上升。
博通⾸席执⾏官陈福阳周⼀在新加坡相关活动上表⽰，资本可能成为制约 AI ⾏业增⻓的最⼤因素
之⼀。这⼀表态凸显出 AI 投资热潮⾯临的现实问题：即便算⼒需求依然强劲，资本供给和资⾦价
格也可能限制建设速度。
与此同时，投资者还需要判断， AI 基础设施投⼊究竟能否持续转化为收⼊和利润。数据中⼼、芯
⽚和电⼒基础设施需要⻓期投⼊，⽽相关收益能否及时兑现，将影响企业的融资能⼒、现⾦流

和估值。
IMF 周⼆在最新报告中也提醒， AI 相关投资的经济收益可能不及部分市场参与者的预期。倘若投
资持续扩张，但实际盈利和⽣产率提升未能同步跟进，市场对相关资产的重新定价可能进⼀步
加剧。
因此，本轮芯⽚股下跌所反映的，不只是存储⾏业的竞争担忧，也包括整个 AI 交易对资⾦成本和
回报率的敏感度上升。
AI ⾏情并未终结，市场开始考察谁能真正兑现收益
尽管芯⽚股遭遇连续抛售，市场仍未普遍认定 AI 投资周期已经结束。周⼀发布新 AI 模型后，
Meta 股价逆市收涨约 3.4% ，显⽰投资者对具体技术进展和产品竞争⼒的判断，并未与整个半导
体板块完全同步。
新加坡资产管理公司 Lucerne Asset Management 的投资主管 Marc Velan 认为，韩国股市的巨震
更多反映了仓位调整和资本成本问题，⽽⾮ AI 需求基本⾯已经恶化。他判断， AI 周期可能正在进
⼊下⼀阶段，投资者将越来越关注商业变现能⼒和投⼊资本回报率，⽽不是单纯押注基础设施
扩张。
这也解释了为什么当前的芯⽚股抛售不能简单等同于 AI 需求崩塌。 AI 算⼒需求是否继续增⻓、基

础设施投资能否获得合理回报，以及存储⾏业竞争是否会压低利润率，是彼此相关但并不相同
的问题。
对市场⽽⾔，接下来的关键不只是 AI 企业还会投⼊多少资⾦，更是这些投⼊能否转化为收⼊、利
润和现⾦流。明星 IPO 搁浅、融资成本上升、债券波动加剧，以及⻓鑫技术进展带来的竞争预期
变化，共同构成了这轮交易逻辑转变的背景。
AI 投资热潮未必已经结束，但单纯依靠增⻓预期推⾼估值的阶段，正⾯临更严格的检验。当资本
成本上升、竞争格局变化和商业回报不确定性同时进⼊定价，全球芯⽚股的调整或许正是市场
重新衡量 AI 投资⻛险与收益的⼀个信号。

### Page 5

Document 181: Australian Business Conditions and Confidence, September (2026-10-14)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Australian Business Conditions and Confidence, September (2026-10-14).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-14
Pages: 12
Status: ok

### Page 1

#businessconditions #confidence #aud #australia
Australian Business Conditions and Confidence, September
By Luka Belobrajdic
Economist, Westpac
14:15 October 13 2026 Share
Confidence posts its sharpest fall since the start of the Middle East conflict as elevated fuel
prices and higher interest rates weigh on business sentiment, while conditions hold steady
at weak levels. Conditions unchanged at -1 / Confidence -4pts to -13.

### Page 2

The September NAB Business Survey was in the field from 23 to 30 September, capturing ongoing
global uncertainty, still-rising fuel costs and, at least in part, the RBA’s September rate hike. Business
confidence continued to deteriorate, recording its sharpest fall since March and the onset of the Middle
East conflict. The index declined 4pts to -13 and, while remaining above the lows recorded earlier this
year, is now at its weakest level since May. At 18pts below its long-run average, the result underscores

the deep pessimism that continues to weigh on businesses. This broadly mirrors the pessimism evident
in the Westpac-Melbourne Institute Consumer Sentiment Index, where elevated fuel prices and higher
interest rates have coalesced in a similarly weak assessment of the outlook.
The headline business conditions index held steady in negative territory at -1, albeit rising 0.25pts on
an unrounded basis. While encouraging that conditions have not deteriorated further, the index has

now remained at -1 (rounded) for two consecutive months, its weakest level since the height of the
COVID-19 pandemic in 2020. At 7pts below its long-run average, the result points to continued
pressure across the business sector.
Across the major sub-components, employment moved into negative territory, falling 3pts to -1 and
sitting 4pts below its long-run average. This marks the first outright negative reading since November
2020 and provides further evidence of the gradual easing in labour market conditions. Against the

backdrop of compressed margins, the result may also indicate firms are becoming increasingly
cautious around hiring decisions. By contrast, trading conditions rose 1pt to 4, although they remain
8pts below their long-run average. The relative resilience in trading conditions is broadly consistent with

### Page 3

Westpac DataX Card-Tracker data, which suggests nominal spending remained resilient, albeit
slowing, through the September quarter despite ongoing headwinds facing consumers. Profitability
recorded the strongest improvement across the major components, rising 3pts to -5, although the sub-
index remains deeply subdued at 10pts below its long-run average.
Capacity utilisation edged 0.2ppt lower to 82.3%. While the monthly decline provides a tentative signal

of easing capacity pressures, utilisation remains 0.9ppt above its long-run average and continues to
trend higher on a three-month average basis. Capital expenditure continued to recover from its mid-
year weakness, rising 3pts to 7. While still below the highs recorded in the first quarter, the sub-index is
now trending higher on a three-month average basis, pointing to some stabilisation in investment
intentions.
Cost growth was comparatively subdued in September, although purchase cost pressures remain

elevated. Purchase cost growth edged 0.1ppt higher to 2.4%qtr, sitting 1.1ppt above its long-run
average and continuing to weigh on margins. By contrast, labour cost growth eased 0.1ppt to 1.7%qtr,
although it remains 0.5ppt above its long-run average. Final product price growth was unchanged at
0.8%qtr, 0.2ppt above its long-run average. The widening gap between purchase cost and final product
price growth suggests firms continue to absorb a meaningful share of higher input costs, consistent

with profitability remaining deeply below average. Looking ahead, the October survey will provide an
early indication of how the card surcharging ban is affecting business costs and the extent to which
these are passed through to final prices.

### Page 4

Related articles
Among other indicators, forward orders declined 1pt to -1, pointing to continued caution around the
demand outlook. Inventories, by contrast, rose 4pts (rounded) to 7, some 5pts above their long-run
average. In combination with weak forward orders, the inventory build points to a degree of slack
emerging across the business sector.

tuần trước với giá trị -1,634 tỷ đồng, tập trung ở các mã VPB, CTG và VCB; đồng thời, tự

Sản lượng hàng qua cảng GMD tiếp 
tục duy trì đà tăng trưởng bền vững

Giá dịch vụ kỳ vọng duy trì đà tăng 
ổn định từ nay đến 2030

Khuyến ngh ị MUA – Giá m ục tiêu 
92,500 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Doanh thu thuần của GMD 2Q2026 đạt 1,762 tỷ VND (+18% yoy). Tổng sản lượng qua 
hệ thống cảng GMD đ ạt 1.35 tri ệu Teu (+7% yoy). LNTT 2Q2026 của GMD ghi nh ận 
1,550 tỷ VND, tăng m ạnh 129% yoy do ghi nh ận khoản lãi tài chính 60 9 tỷ VND t ừ 
chuyển nhượng cổ phần công ty liên kết. Lũy kế 6 tháng đầu năm 2026, doanh thu và 
LNTT của GMD lần lượt hoàn thành 49%/81% doanh thu và LNTT kế hoạch 2026.

Sản lượng hàng qua h ệ thống cảng GMD k ỳ vọng đạt tăng trưởng 11.6% yoy trong 
2026 và CAGR 9%/năm giai đo ạn 2026 –2030. 2H2026, Nam Đình Vũ đư ợc k ỳ vọng 
phục hồi nhờ có thêm 2 tuyến dịch vụ mới, trong khi Gemalink ti ếp tục tăng trưởng 
nhờ bổ sung tuyến mới và ngu ồn hàng ổn định từ hãng tàu đ ối tác. Triển vọng dài 
hạn được củng cố bởi dư đ ịa công su ất tại Nam Đình Vũ 3 và Gemalink 2  trong bối 
cảnh nguồn cung Cái Mép đến 2030 dự kiến vẫn thấp hơn nhu cầu.

Giá dịch vụ tại các cảng GMD kỳ vọng duy trì đà tăng trung bình 5 –7%/năm tại phía 
Bắc và 7-10%/năm tại phía Nam giai đoạn 2026–2030, được hỗ trợ bởi (1) dư địa tăng 
giá còn lớn so với các nước khác trong khu vực và (2) khung trần giá dịch vụ tại cảng 
nước sâu vừa được Bộ Xây dựng nâng 10% từ 01/02/2026.

Chúng tôi khuy ến ngh ị MUA đ ối với cổ phiếu GMD, giá m ục tiêu là 92,500 VND/cổ 
phiếu, cao hơn 16.4% so với mức giá đóng cửa ngày 28/08/2026.

KQKD 6M2026 tăng trưởng khả quan 
so với cùng kỳ

HRC là động lực chính thúc đ ẩy tăng 
trưởng sản lượng tiêu thụ thép

Kỳ vọng giá bán thép có xu hư ớng 
được điều chỉnh tăng sau Q3/2026

Tiếp tục nâng cao kh ả năng sản xuất 
để nắm bắt các cơ hội mới

Khuyến ngh ị MUA, giá m ục tiêu 
32,150 VND/cổ phiếu 
 HPG ghi nhận doanh thu và LNST (loại trừ thu nhập từ chuyển nhượng dự án) 6M2026 
đạt lần lượt 108,060/11,538 t ỷ VND, tăng 47%/52% svck. Đ ộng lực tăng trư ởng chủ 
yếu đến từ: (1) tiến độ giải ngân đầu tư công đư ợc đẩy mạnh, (2) các d ự án hạ tầng 
quy mô l ớn bước vào giai đo ạn thi công, (3) các bi ện pháp thu ế quan giúp thu h ẹp 
chênh lệch giá giữa HRC nội địa và nhập khẩu.

Chúng tôi dự báo tổng sản lượng tiêu thụ thép năm 2026/2027 đạt lần lượt 14.2/15.5 
triệu tấn. Lũy kế 6M2026, sản lượng bán hàng c ủa Hòa Phát đ ạt 6.8 tri ệu tấn, trong 
đó s ản lượng HRC đ ạt 3.4 tri ệu tấn tăng 57% svck, nh ờ Dung Qu ất 2 đã v ận hành 
khoảng 92% công suất thiết kế và mảng thép xây dựng tăng 17% svck nhưng các dây 
chuyền hiện tại đã hoạt động hết công suất.

Do xung đ ột chính tr ị và thắt ch ặt ngu ồn cung than c ốc, các doanh nghi ệp trong 
ngành đã điều chỉnh giá bán thép xây dựng và HRC bình quân lần lượt +8%/+12% để 
giảm áp lực lên biên lợi nhuận. KBSV kỳ vọng giá thép tăng trở lại từ Q4/2026 khi các 
doanh nghiệp trong ngành tăng cường nhập hàng để phục vụ nhu cầu xây dựng và 
tiến độ thi công đẩy nhanh vào giai đoạn cuối năm.

Hòa Phát đang triển khai đầu tư các d ự án lớn, trong đó bao g ồm (1) các h ạng mục 
thuộc Khu liên hợp Dung Quất, (2) dự án nhà máy thép ray và thép đặc biệt công suất 
700,000 tấn/năm, và (3) d ự án container. Chúng tôi gi ữ quan điểm tích c ực về triển 
vọng mở rộng danh m ục sản phẩm giá tr ị gia tăng của HPG, song v ẫn cần theo dõi 
thêm tiến độ vận hành, sản lượng thực tế và năng lực cạnh tranh được phân tích dưới 
báo cáo.

KBSV duy trì khuyến nghị MUA với giá mục tiêu 32,150 VND/cổ phiếu, tương ứng mức 
lợi nhuận kỳ vọng 48.2% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 04/09/2026.

TCT Cảng Hàng không Việt Nam (ACV) 
Tăng trưởng chậm lại trước chu kỳ đầu tư mới 
07/09/2026  
Chuyên viên cao cấp Nguyễn Ngọc Anh 
(+84) 24-7303-5333 anhntn@kbsec.com.vn

KQKD tăng trư ởng chậm l ại trong 
2Q2026

Lượt khách qua cảng hàng không 
Việt Nam duy trì tăng trưởng bền 
vững giai đoạn từ nay đến 2030

Số lượt khách quốc tế/nội địa của 
ACV dự kiến đạt tăng trưởng trung 
bình 6.8/3.3%/năm giai đoạn 2026-
2030

Long Thành 1 đang được đẩy nhanh 
tiến độ để kịp khai thác từ đầu 2027

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP, 
giá mục tiêu 46,600 VND/cổ phiếu

2Q2026, ACV ghi nhận doanh thu đạt 6,324 tỷ VND (-0.3% yoy), chủ yếu do (1) ACV đã 
hoàn thành chuy ển nhượng Cảng hàng không (CHK) Phú Qu ốc cho Sun Group vào 
1/1/2026 và (2) ảnh hưởng từ việc cắt giảm đường bay Trung Đông. Lũy k ế 6 tháng 
đầu năm 2026, ACV ghi nhận doanh thu và LNST công ty mẹ đạt 13,164/5,278 tỷ VND 
(+4%/12% yoy).

Chúng tôi d ự phóng lư ợt khách hàng không Vi ệt Nam tăng trung bình kho ảng 
9%/năm giai đoạn 2026–2030 nhờ nền kinh tế duy trì tăng trưởng cao, các hãng hàng 
không mở rộng đội bay, mạng bay và công suất sân bay được bổ sung liên tục, với đà 
tăng được kỳ vọng cải thiện từ 2H2026 khi áp lực từ xung đột Trung Đông và giá nhiên 
liệu giảm bớt.

Tăng trưởng hành khách quốc tế và nội địa thông qua hệ thống cảng hàng không của 
ACV dự kiến đạt CAGR lần lượt 6.8% và 3.3% giai đoạn 2026–2030, thấp hơn mức tăng 
trưởng chung của toàn thị trường hàng không Việt Nam do một phần tăng trưởng lưu 
lượng hành khách sẽ được hấp thụ bởi các sân bay ngoài hệ thống ACV trong những 
năm tới, bao gồm dự án Cảng hàng không Gia Bình và Phú Quốc.

Dự án Cảng HKQT Long Thành 1 đang đư ợc đẩy nhanh tiến độ dù một số hạng mục 
vẫn còn khối lượng lớn, dự kiến hoàn thành và bắt đầu khai thác thương mại từ đầu 
2027, bổ sung công suất 25 triệu khách/năm cho ACV.

Dựa trên tri ển vọng kinh doanh và k ết quả định giá, chúng tôi khuy ến nghị TRUNG 
LẬP đối với cổ phiếu ACV, giá mục tiêu là 46,600 VND/cổ phiếu, cao hơn 13.7% so với 
giá đóng cửa ngày 04/09/2026.

Tăng trưởng tín dụng được dự báo 
tiếp tục duy trì tích cực

NIM cả năm 2026, được kỳ vọng vẫn 
cải thiện nhẹ so với 2025

Chất lư ợng tài s ản suy gi ảm nh ẹ 
nhưng vẫn trong tầm kiểm soát

Khuyến ngh ị: MUA – Giá m ục tiêu 
26,300 VNĐ/CP  
 Chúng tôi duy trì dự phóng tăng trưởng tín dụng 2026 đạt 15%, với động lực thúc đẩy 
chính đến từ dư nợ phân khúc KHDN, đặc biệt là nhóm KHDN lớn và FDI, mặc dù nhu 
cầu đi vay của phân khúc bán l ẻ trong năm dự kiến sẽ chậm hơn do ảnh hưởng của 
mặt bằng lãi suất cao.

NIM có thể bị ảnh hưởng phần nào do tình hình huy động thị trường 1 chưa khả quan 
buộc ngân hàng phải phụ thuộc vào kênh giấy tờ có giá với chi phí cao hơn trong nửa 
cuối năm. Dù vậy, chúng tôi kỳ vọng NIM của ngân hàng vẫn có thể cải thiện nhẹ so 
với cuối năm 2025 nhờ (1) ngân hàng đẩy mạnh cho vay trung dài hạn với lợi suất tốt 
hơn; (2) danh mục cho vay tiếp tục được tái định giá ở mức cao hơn trong các quý tới; 
và (3) ngân hàng có thêm nguồn huy động offshore với chi phí vốn hợp lý.

Chất lượng tài sản trong 2026 dự kiến suy giảm nhẹ, phản ánh môi trường lãi suất 
cho vay tăng cao. Chúng tôi duy trì dự phóng tỷ lệ NPL sẽ tăng 13bps so với 2025 và 
tăng dự phóng đối với chi phí trích lập dự phòng so với báo cáo kỳ trước do tác 
động của Nghị định 86 lên hoạt động trích lập của ngân hàng trong nửa đầu năm 
mạnh hơn nhận định trước đó.

Dựa trên triển vọng kinh doanh của ngân hàng cũng như đánh giá các y ếu tố rủi ro, 
chúng tôi tiếp tục duy trì khuyến nghị MUA với cổ phiếu ACB với giá mục tiêu 26,300 
VNĐ/CP - tiềm năng tăng giá 18% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 08/09/2026.

Khuyến ngh ị: MUA – Giá m ục tiêu 
38,750 VNĐ/CP  
 Chúng tôi nâng dự phóng tăng trưởng tín dụng của ngân hàng trong giai đoạn 
2026F-2028F lên khoảng 18-19% nhờ (1) hưởng lợi từ chính sách nới tín dụng đối 
với các dự án hạ tầng trọng điểm, (2) mối quan hệ chiến lược với các nhà thầu lớn, 
và (3) chủ động đa dạng nguồn huy động để đáp ứng nhu cầu tín dụng.

Với luận điểm ngân hàng s ẽ đẩy mạnh cho vay các d ự án hạ tầng thuộc chủ trương 
của Chính phủ và mặt bằng lãi suất đang neo ở mức cao, chúng tôi cho r ằng NIM sẽ 
tiếp tục chịu áp lực trong năm 2026, trước khi cải thiện dần trong các năm tiếp theo 
khi áp lực lãi suất hạ nhiệt và thu nhập lãi từ các khoản vay thuộc chính sách Flexible 
Pricing sẽ được thu hồi.

Chất lượng tài sản dự kiến suy giảm nhẹ, phản ánh môi trường lãi suất neo cao gây 
áp lực lên danh mục bán lẻ của ngân hàng. Chúng tôi nâng dự phóng đối với tỷ lệ 
NPL lên thêm 10bps so với báo cáo kỳ trước và nâng nhẹ chi phí dự phòng với kỳ 
vọng ngân hàng sẽ tiếp tục duy trì bộ đệm an toàn ở mức cao.

Dựa trên triển vọng kinh doanh của ngân hàng cũng như đánh giá các y ếu tố rủi ro, 
chúng tôi ti ếp tục duy trì khuy ến ngh ị MUA đối với cổ phiếu TCB v ới giá m ục tiêu

Khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP , giá mục tiêu 
56,700 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Doanh thu thuần 2Q2026 đạt 5,472 tỷ VND (+10% yoy), trong khi LNST đạt 73 tỷ 
VND, giảm 25% yoy do biên lợi nhuận thu hẹp đến từ (1) tăng chi phí nhiên liệu và 
(2) tăng chi phí vận hành, khấu hao cho các dự án đầu tư mới. Lũy kế 6T2026, 
doanh thu và LNST của VTP lần lượt đạt 10,230/112 tỷ VND, hoàn thành 52%/28% 
doanh thu và LNST kế hoạch của 2026.

Chúng tôi dự phóng sản lượng chuyển phát Việt Nam tăng 22% yoy trong 2026 và duy 
trì CAGR 15% giai đoạn 2026–2030 nhờ nhu cầu giao nhận tiếp tục mở rộng theo đà 
tăng trưởng của thị trường TMĐT . VTP kỳ vọng tiếp tục gia tăng th ị phần lên 23.5% 
năm 2026 và đ ạt 25.5% vào 2030, duy trì t ốc độ tăng trưởng sản lượng cao hơn th ị 
trường.

VTP đang đ ẩy nhanh đ ầu tư các trung tâm logistics t ại Lạng Sơn, Hưng Yên và Đà 
Nẵng, đồng thời mở rộng hoạt động tại Lào, Campuchia và Trung Qu ốc nhằm từng 
bước hoàn thiện mạng lưới logistics xuyên biên gi ới, tạo nền tảng tham gia sâu hơn 
vào chuỗi logistics toàn trình. Chúng tôi d ự phóng doanh thu kho vận và vận tải đạt 
CAGR 22%, trong khi logistics xuyên biên gi ới tăng trung bình 29%/năm giai đo ạn 
2026–2030.

thành của một mẫu nến phân phối tiêu cực trên khung tuần. Trên khung ngày, 2 phiên 
giảm mạnh vào đầu và cuối tuần cũng cho thấy áp lực bán mang tính áp đảo với độ rộng 
thị trường hoàn toàn nghiêng về số mã giảm điểm. Với đà phân phối hiện tại, áp lực điều

góc nhìn rộng hơn, xu hướng trung hạn có phần trung tính khi chỉ số đang hình thành các 
mẫu hình vận động đi ngang theo tam giác hoặc hình bình hành.

Document 204: China Memory HSBC 260930 smartphone

https://www.research.hsbc.com 
The 25th edition of the EM Sentiment Survey
Click to view
Less bull, more bite

◆ Smartphone companies have gone from being demand drivers 
to price takers 
◆ But we think if memory price inflation slows, that could be 
enough for margins to improve before shipments increase 
◆ In a separate report published today, we initiate coverage of 
Xiaomi with a Buy rating and a TP of HKD33.20 
Hope amid hard times. This report looks at what component shortages and higher 
memory prices mean for the economics of the smartphone market. The key message 
is that while average selling prices have jumped as costs surged, handset makers 
don’t need memory prices to fall to recover. Any slowdown in AI-driven memory price 
inflation could be enough for margins to improve before shipments start to increase.  
Stabilisation in 2027, recovery in 2028. IDC forecasts that global smartphone 
shipments will fall 16.7% y-o-y in 2026 – the steepest contraction in industry history – 
decline a further 2.9% in 2027, and rebound 4.5% y-o-y in 2028.  The low base in 2026 
should release some replacement demand but higher handset ASPs and a limited supply 
of low-end memory chips will likely limit the recovery. In China, handset inventories rose 
in 2H25 and 1Q26 before easing in 2Q, suggesting the shock of higher memory costs 
arrived before inventory and demand had normalised. We expect the first green shoots to 
appear in ASPs and gross margin, ahead of shipment numbers. 
Memory inflation hit margins before consumer prices. Spot pricing moves first, 
followed by contract prices, handset makers’ blended procurement costs, component 
inventory consumption, handset bill of materials (BOM) and reported gross margin. 
Lower-cost memory inventory delays the impact on earnings, while finished goods 
and channel inventories delay the ability to reprice. We see 4Q25-1Q26 as the 
margin-absorption phase, with the larger earnings impact likely to run through 2H26.  
Higher costs to be passed on through new products. The main levers for handset 
makers are higher prices for new models, fewer discounts, slower RAM and storage 
upgrades, wider premiums for high-memory models and savings elsewhere in the BOM. 
Times are particularly tough because AI/server demand is tightening memory supply at a 
time when smartphone demand remains weak. In previous cycles, smartphones and PCs 
were major sources of DRAM and NAND demand, so higher memory costs coincided 
with better end demand. This time, handset makers are having to accept supplier-driven 
price increases without the same support from sales volumes.  
MLCC risk. AI servers use more and higher-spec multi-layer ceramic capacitors 
(MLCCs), shifting supply to higher-value products. The effect on handset profits should be 
limited as these key components make up only a small part of the BOM. However, supply 
shortages can still disrupt production – if consumer-grade components are scarce, finding 
replacements takes time. See our Xiaomi initiation report.
30 September 2026

Why we think the smartphone market won’t recover in 2027 
The decline in 2026 will not create a strong replacement cycle next year 
According to IDC forecasts made in June 2026, global smartphone shipments will fall 16.7%  
y-o-y in 2026e to just over 1bn units, decline a further 2.9% in 2027e, and then rebound 4.5% in 
2028e when memory supply begins to normalise.  
The low base should support replacement demand, but affordability remains the constraint. Higher 
memory costs are raising the bill of materials (BOM) for handsets and pushing manufacturers to 
either increase prices or reduce entry-level supply. The thinking is that a consumer who defers an 
upgrade in 2026 does not necessarily return in 2027 if the replacement device is materially more 
expensive, or offers less memory at the same price. We think premium markets should be more 
resilient, while entry-level Android demand is more exposed.

Exhibit 2. Global smartphone shipments: 
recovery pushed out to 2028e 
 Exhibit 3. China smartphone inventories* 
were still elevated when memory costs 
began to rise

Source: IDC estimates, HSBC  *Inventory proxy: China’s smartphone production + imports – China’s smartphone 
shipment – exports. 
Source: NBS, GAC, CAICT, HSBC

The first-half vendor data already show this split. Global shipments declined 5.5% y-o-y in 1H26. 
Apple and Samsung shipments rose 5.8% and 8.7%, respectively, while those for Xiaomi, 
OPPO and Vivo fell 22.8%, 13.8% and 13.5%, respectively (source: IDC). Memory is not the 
only explanation: product timing, geography, channel execution and inventory also matter. 
However, the divergence is consistent with stronger resilience at the premium end and greater 
volume pressure in price-sensitive Android segments.  
China’s implied handset-inventory series point in the same direction. Inventory rose through 
2H25 and reached another high in 1Q26 before easing in 2Q26. The series is calculated as 
production plus imports less domestic shipments and exports. It should be treated as a 
directional supply-chain proxy rather than actual channel inventory because the underlying 
datasets use different definitions and timing. The turning point is nevertheless useful: 
manufacturers entered the memory upcycle before the finished-goods cycle had fully normalised. 
Memory pressure extends beyond smartphones, but the earnings sensitivity varies 
materially by product 
TVs have seen a meaningful increase in memory’s share of BOM as prices rose, while 
wearables, audio devices and other IoT products generally carry lower absolute memory 
content and therefore less direct P&L sensitivity. In these categories, selected niche-memory 
availability and channel inventory & pricing can matter more than headline DRAM or NAND 
moves. We are beginning to see signs that pricing momentum is moderating in some consumer 
and niche categories, although conditions remain tight in higher-capacity products. Our sector

call is therefore about the second derivative: slower memory inflation is likely to moderate

Why memory inflation is not the sole cause of the fall in smartphone demand 
Xiaomi’s historical shipment data suggests that the relationship is weak. Mobile DRAM prices 
have moved through several cycles since 2018, while Xiaomi’s quarterly shipments have been 
driven more visibly by product launches and seasonality. Falling memory prices have not 
consistently triggered stronger shipments, while rising prices have not resulted in an immediate 
or proportionate decline in units.

This does not mean memory prices are irrelevant with regards to demand. Consumers do not
replace a phone because DRAM or NAND becomes cheaper, and they do not automatically
cancel a replacement because memory becomes more expensive. Replacement age, macro
conditions, product innovation, subsidies, retail financing and channel inventory remain the main
drivers of unit sales. Memory prices affect demand indirectly once higher component costs

change retail prices, available specifications, or the supply of low-end models.
What is different this time is the source of the price increase. In earlier cycles, smartphones and
PCs were large contributors to incremental DRAM and NAND demand. The same healthy end
demand that tightened memory supply also helped handset makers raise ASP and improve mix.
In 2026, AI servers and high bandwidth memory (HBM) are pulling capacity and capital towards
higher-return applications, while consumer electronics demand remains weak.

TrendForce, an industry research company based in Taiwan, expects overall mobile DRAM price
hikes of around 8-13% q-o-q in 3Q26, given Samsung’s already elevated base in 2Q26. The supply
of low-power double data rate (LPDDR) RAM, a specialized type of computer memory designed to
use less power and take up less physical space than standard RAM, is still tight.
In terms of NAND, TrendForce expects price increases of 10-15% in 3Q26, with contract prices

already at record highs. As a result, we believe that weaker smartphone production may not be
enough to bring memory prices down quickly. AI/server demand can continue to absorb
available supply, leaving handset makers with less bargaining power than in a consumer
electronics-led cycle.
When are higher memory prices reflected in reported earnings?
The simple answer is later than suggested by the spot price chart below (Exhibit 8), and with no
fixed lag. Large smartphone OEMs procure most memory under contract rather than buying

directly in the spot market. Spot prices affect negotiations and indicate direction, but the
relevant cost is the OEM's blended contract price across suppliers, memory generations and
purchase periods.
The transmission path: Spot price → contract price → OEM procurement cost → component
inventory consumption → handset BOM → reported gross margin.
Rolling inventory delays the transmission in both directions. When contract prices rise, lower-
cost DRAM and NAND inventory can temporarily protect reported margin. When memory prices

stabilise or fall, more expensive inventory may still flow through the cost of goods sold (COGS)
before the savings become visible. Existing finished goods and channel inventory can delay the
impact further, as OEMs may need to sell older models before new launches can reset prices
and specifications.
Exhibit 8 is consistent with a lagged but not fixed transmission of memory costs. Mobile DRAM
prices began declining in 3Q22 and Xiaomi’s smartphone margin subsequently improved, while

the increase in mobile DRAM prices from 3Q24 was followed by weaker margins in 2025. This
sequence is consistent with inventory-led cost transmission, but it does not establish a structural
asymmetry or a fixed 2-3 quarters lag. Product mix, promotion, channel inventory and other
component costs also affect Xiaomi’s reported gross margin.
We believe 4Q25-1Q26 likely marked the initial absorption period, when contract price began
resetting but lower-cost inventory still provided some protection. The impact of higher

procurement costs should become more visible in 2H26 as inventories turn, although the timing
and magnitude would depend on Xiaomi’s procurement contracts, inventory position, shipment
mix and product launch schedule. Margins could improve in 2027e if memory prices stabilise
and new products provide greater scope for pricing, specs and mix adjustments.

MLCCs: smaller cost, but a potentially larger operational risk 
Multi-layer ceramic capacitors (MLCCs), the components that store and release electrical 
energy to stabilize voltage, are a key part of the story. AI demand is creating a more selective 
MLCC squeeze than the broad-based memory inflation discussed above. AI servers require 
substantially more MLCC content and a greater mix of high-capacitance and higher-
specification parts than conventional servers. This does not mean smartphones directly 
compete for every component: the AI-grade components where supply is tightest and 
mainstream handset MLCCs often have different specifications. 
Instead, the transmission mechanism is through capacity allocation and supply-chain behaviour. 
Japanese and Korean suppliers are increasingly prioritising higher-value AI and infrastructure 
products, while longer lead times, distributor restocking and precautionary ordering are 
beginning to spill over into some consumer-grade categories. Recent industry data point to a 
bifurcated market – tight high-end supply alongside a broader, but still uneven, tightening in 
mainstream products.  
For smartphone handset makers, the direct earnings exposure is much smaller than for memory 
because MLCC accounts for only a small share of BOM. The operational risk, however, can be 
disproportionate. MLCCs are low-cost but specification- and qualification-sensitive components; 
replacing an unavailable part may require supplier qualification or board-level validation. A 
shortage in a relatively inexpensive component can delay production even when its impact on 
BOM cost is immaterial. 
We view memory primarily as an earnings and affordability bottleneck, while MLCC represents a 
second-order production risk. The key factor to watch is whether today’s tight supply of high-end 
components broadens into sustained shortages of qualified consumer-grade parts. 
Premium brands have more room to pass through costs; mass-
market brands have more margin exposure 
Can Xiaomi pass through higher memory costs? 
We believe the company is unlikely to pass through the full increase without affecting demand. 
Xiaomi’s lower handset ASP leaves a smaller gross profit pool per device, while competition in 
mass-market price bands limits the scope for headline price increases. Xiaomi can partly offset 
the pressure through premiumisation, specification adjustments and lower promotional intensity, 
but it will likely absorb part of the higher cost through smartphone gross margin.

Our bottom-up estimates show that memory represents a meaningful and rising share of 
Xiaomi’s handset cost base (Exhibit 9). For the selected Xiaomi models in our sample, 
estimated memory cost was generally equivalent to approximately 6-16% of handset COGS 
before 2024. Under our mobile DRAM and NAND contract price assumptions, the ratio rises to 
approximately 35% for the model selected in 2026 (Mi 18 Pro). Selected iPhone models are 
included as a benchmark: the corresponding ratio reaches approximately 25% in 2026e, as the 
iPhone’s higher selling price provides a larger cost and gross profit buffer.

The comparison does not suggest that Xiaomi necessarily uses more memory per device. 
Rather, a similar dollar increase in DRAM and NAND cost has a larger proportional impact on a 
lower-priced model. Xiaomi could raise prices to protect unit economics, but this risks 
weakening demand in price-sensitive markets. It could also reduce memory specifications or 
promotional intensity, although these measures may affect product competitiveness. 
Premiumisation provides the most constructive offset, but is unlikely to fully neutralise the near-
term cost increase across Xiaomi’s portfolio. 
Pass-through likely to come through new product launches 
Increasing the official price of an existing handset is usually impractical because the product 
already has a retail reference point and channel inventory. The adjustment instead takes place 
at the next launch. 
◆ The first lever is the launch price and effective ASP. OEMs can raise the next 
generation's starting price, reduce launch subsidies, cut discounts or push demand toward 
Pro and Ultra variants. The nominal entry price can remain unchanged while realised ASP 
rises because the base model receives less promotion or has lower availability. 
◆ The second lever is memory specification. Upgrades from 8GB to 12GB of RAM or from 
256GB to 512GB of storage can be delayed. The base SKU can retain a smaller 
configuration while premium models continue to upgrade. TrendForce still expects average 
smartphone DRAM capacity to reach 8.5GB in 2026, up 10% y-o-y, but the average masks 
a larger split between premium and low-end models (source: TrendForce, May 2026). 
◆ The third lever is stock keeping unit (SKU) structure. OEMs can widen the price gap 
between 256GB and 512GB products, remove a low-priced high-memory version, or 
reserve newer memory technology for flagship models. This recovers more of the cost from 
less price-sensitive buyers. 
◆ The fourth lever is cost reduction elsewhere. OEMs can press camera, display, mechanical 
and other suppliers for savings, trim other specifications, reduce promotional spending or 
accept temporary margin pressure. Memory inflation can become a pricing-pressure event for 
the wider handset supply chain even if the official handset price changes only modestly.

### Page 8

Equities ● Electronic Equipment & Instruments 
30 September 2026 
8 
2027: margins to recover before unit sales 
By 2027, most OEMs should have completed one or two launch cycles under the higher 
memory-cost regime. This should allow higher effective ASP, narrower discounts, redesigned 
memory tiers and lower low-end exposure to stabilise gross margin. IDC's forecast of a further 
2.9% shipment decline is therefore compatible with better earnings. 
For Xiaomi, the key question is whether new launches restore smartphone gross margin without 
causing a disproportionate loss of unit shipments. Our base case is for a slight decrease in  
shipments in 2027e, while ASP continues to improve and gross margin stabilises. The bull case 
would require memory supply to normalise earlier, allowing OEMs to restore lower-priced 
products while deferred replacement demand is released. The bear case is persistent LPDDR 
tightness combined with weaker consumer demand, forcing another round of production cuts, 
specification reductions and margin absorption. 
The bottom line 
Global smartphone shipments are unlikely to recover meaningfully in 2027, but OEM earnings 
could improve as new product cycles enable greater cost pass-through and margin 
normalisation. For Xiaomi, the reported earnings recovery will depend on inventory turnover and 
execution on pricing and mix, while the equity market may anticipate the inflection before it 
becomes visible in reported margins.

Document 268: How AI agents could reshape retail as stores move beyond sales

Source PDF: all report pdf/How AI agents could reshape retail as stores move beyond sales.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-08
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

Agentic commerce, where autonomous AI agents
independently select and purchase products on behalf of
consumers, is forcing retailers to rethink their role. As AI takes
over more of the buying process, physical stores are evolving
from a sales channel into a destination for advice, service,
experience, and omnichannel support
Katinka Jongkind
A Galaxy General AI robot retail store, Shanghai. Retail is entering a new phase
of digital disruption, where agentic commerce could fundamentally change

how consumers shop
Agentic commerce fundamentally changes
the buying process
Retail is entering a new phase of digital disruption. E-commerce
has already transformed where consumers shop; agentic
commerce is set to change how they shop. Autonomous artiﬁcial
intelligence agents can search, compare and purchase products on
behalf of consumers, shifting shopping from an active task to an
increasingly automated background process.
Cookie settings

### Page 2

Agentic commerce goes beyond AI
assistants and comparison websites
Key diﬀerences between agentic commerce, AI assistants (such as
ChatGPT and Gemini) and comparison websites
Source: ING Research
Most relevant for routine purchases, less so
for luxury goods
Agentic commerce is not equally suited to every product category.
Its greatest potential lies in repeat and replacement purchases, as
well as functional, standardised products. In these categories, AI

agents can eﬃciently compare prices, speciﬁcations, and
availability, while using consumption patterns to determine the
optimal time to buy.
Adoption is likely to be slower for luxury, trend-sensitive and
experience-driven products. In these categories, inspiration,
emotion, and personal preference remain central to the purchasing
decision, making consumers less inclined to relinquish control. AI
agents can assist by pre-selecting products that match predeﬁned

preferences, but ﬁnal purchasing decisions are likely to remain
ﬁrmly in the hands of the consumer.

### Page 3

Consumer electronics and personal care
lead the adoption of agentic commerce
Within non-food retail, agentic commerce appears particularly well
suited to consumer electronics and personal care. Both sectors are
characterised by a high degree of product standardisation and a
large share of repeat purchases, making them ideal candidates for
AI-driven purchasing.
In consumer electronics, purchasing decisions are often based on
objective and easily comparable criteria, including price, technical

speciﬁcations, and availability. This makes products such as
televisions, laptops and other electronic devices well suited to
automation, allowing AI agents to manage much of the purchasing
journey from product selection to the ﬁnal transaction.
The potential is equally signiﬁcant in personal care, where many
purchases are routine and repetitive. Products such as diapers,
shampoo, and toothpaste can be reordered automatically based
on usage patterns and preset preferences. By reducing the eﬀort

required to order everyday products, AI agents could further
accelerate the shift towards online shopping.
Agentic commerce is best suited to
consumer electronics and personal care
products
Product categories most and least suited to agentic commerce

### Page 4

Source: ING Research
Personal taste limits AI agents' potential in
fashion and living
For now, fashion and home furnishings appear less suited to fully
autonomous agentic commerce. Purchasing decisions in these
categories are often shaped by inspiration, aesthetics, personal
taste, and broader lifestyle considerations, making consumers less
willing to hand over control to an AI agent.
While AI can support product discovery and narrow down options

based on predeﬁned preferences, the ﬁnal purchase decision will
typically remain with the consumer. In these segments, AI is
therefore more likely to serve as a decision-support tool than as a
fully autonomous purchasing agent.
Chinese platforms lead adoption
Agentic commerce creates the greatest opportunities for large
global marketplaces and technology companies. These players
combine vast product assortments with the scale, data and
infrastructure needed to deploy AI agents eﬀectively. Their

integrated ecosystems, including payment solutions, logistics
networks, and data platforms, enable them to integrate AI agents
into their business operations faster and more eﬀectively.
Chinese platforms such as Alibaba and JD.com currently lead the
way, with AI agents already deeply integrated into their respective
platform networks. These agents can not only provide product

### Page 5

recommendations but also complete purchases autonomously.
Meanwhile, US technology companies including Amazon, Google,
Meta, OpenAI and Anthropic are investing heavily in the
development and rollout of AI agents, intensifying the global race
for leadership in this emerging market.
European players are structurally lagging behind their American
and Chinese competitors. Stricter regulation and less integrated
platform networks limit their ability to bundle scale, data and

services. While Zalando is among Europe’s frontrunners in AI-
powered retail, its applications are currently focused on product
discovery, comparison, and selection. Fully autonomous
purchasing has yet to become a reality.
Technology platforms are becoming the
gatekeepers of commerce
AI agents are rarely neutral actors. Embedded within commercial
ecosystems, they are inherently linked to the interests of platforms,
marketplaces and technology companies. Although agentic

commerce promises greater convenience and time savings for
consumers, it is also likely to strengthen the dominance of large
tech ﬁrms and marketplaces. These parties are becoming
increasingly inﬂuential in shaping product choices and controlling
consumer access.
Physical stores are not disappearing, but
their role is changing
How will the role of the physical store evolve in an AI-driven retail
landscape?

### Page 6

Source: ING Research
Redeﬁning the store in the age of AI
Agentic commerce is expected to accelerate the shift of sales to
online channels, putting further pressure on the existence of
physical stores. The extent of this impact will largely depend on
consumers’ willingness to delegate purchasing decisions to AI
agents.
Physical stores will nevertheless remain relevant for many non-food
retailers. Their role is shifting from sales channel to destination for

advice, service, inspiration, and support of online sales. Stores that
continue to rely mainly on transactional sales risk losing relevance
in an increasingly AI-driven retail landscape.
Retailers that successfully integrate their physical and digital
channels, deﬁne clear roles for their stores, and deploy technology
strategically can strengthen their position within the customer
journey. In a future shaped by AI-driven commerce, their role will

increasingly centre on adding value where automation cannot.
